<html> </html>
Creates an HTML document

<head> </head>
Sets off the title & other info that isn't displayed

<body> </body>
Sets off the visible portion of the document

Puts name of the document in the title bar; when
bookmarking pages, this is what is bookmarked
Body attributes (only used in email newsletters)

<body bgcolor=?>
Sets background color, using name or hex value

<body text=?>
Sets text color, using name or hex value

<body link=?>
Sets color of links, using name or hex value

<body vlink=?>
Sets color of visited links, using name or hex value

<body alink=?>
Sets color of active links (while mouse-clicking)
Text Tags



<font color=?> </font>
Sets font color (should use CSS instead)

<font face=?> </font>
Defines the font used (should use CSS instead)
Links

<a href="URL">clickable text</a>
Creates a hyperlink to a Uniform Resource Locator

<a href="mailto:EMAIL_ADDRESS">clickable text</a>
Creates a hyperlink to an email address

<a name="NAME">
Creates a target location within a document

<a href="#NAME">clickable text</a>
Creates a link to that target location
Formatting

<p> </p>
Creates a new paragraph

<br>
AInserts a line break (carriage return)

<blockquote>Puts content in a quote - indents text from both sides </blockquote>


<div>Used to format block content with CSS </div>


<span> Used to format inline content with CSS
Lists</span>


<ul>Creates an unordered list </ul>


<ol start=?> </ol>
Creates an ordered list (start=xx,
where xx is a counting number)

<li>Encompasses each list item </li>


<dl> Creates a definition list</dl>


<dt>Precedes eachdefintion term</dt>

<dd>
 Precedes eachdefintion
 Graphical elements

<hr>
Inserts a horizontal rule

<hr size=?>
Sets size (height) of horizontal rule

<hr width=?>
Sets width of rule (as a % or absolute pixel length)

<hr noshade>
Creates a horizontal rule without a shadow

<img src="URL" />
Adds image; it is a separate file located at the URL

<img src="URL" align=?>
Aligns image left/right/center/bottom/top/middle (use CSS)

<img src="URL" border=?>
Sets size of border surrounding image (use CSS)

<img src="URL" height=?>
Sets height of image, in pixels

<img src="URL" width=?>
Sets width of image, in pixels

<img src="URL" alt=?>
Sets the alternate text for browsers that can't
process images (required by the ADA)

<form>Defines a form </form>


<select multiple name=? size=?> </select>
Creates a scrolling menu. Size sets the number of
menu items visible before user needs to scroll.

<select name=?> </select>
Creates a pulldown menu

<option>
Sets off each menu item

<textarea name=? cols="x" rows="y"></textarea>
Creates a text box area. Columns set the width;
rows set the height.

<input type="checkbox" name=? value=?>
Creates a checkbox.

<input type="checkbox" name=? value=? checked>
Creates a checkbox which is pre-checked.

<input type="radio" name=? value=?>
Creates a radio button.

<input type="radio" name=? value=? checked>
Creates a radio button which is pre-checked.

<input type="text" name=? size=?>
Creates a one-line text area. Size sets length, in characters.

<input type="submit" value=?>
Creates a submit button. Value sets the text in the submit button.

<input type="image" name=? src=? border=? alt=?>
Creates a submit button using an image. 

<input type="reset">
Creates a reset button


Tables (use only for data layout - use CSS for page layout) Table attributes (only use for email newsletters)

<table> Creates a table </table>


<tr>Sets off each row in a table </tr>


<td> Sets off each cell in a row</td>


<th>Sets off the table header (a normal cell with bold, centered text) </th>


<!-- <table border=?> -->
    <table border=80%>
<!-- Sets the width of the border around table cells
 -->
 
<table cellspacing=?>
Sets amount of space between table cells

<table cellpadding=?>
Sets amount of space between a cell's border and
its contents

<table width=?>
Sets width of the table in pixels or as a percentage

<tr align=?>
Sets alignment for cells within the row
(left/center/right)

<td align=?>
Sets alignment for cells (left/center/right)

<tr valign=?>
Sets vertical alignment for cells within the row
(top/middle/bottom)

<td valign=?>
Sets vertical alignment for cell (top/middle/bottom)

<td rowspan=?>
Sets number of rows a cell should span (default=1)

<td colspan=?>
Sets number of columns a cell should span
🫵🫵🫵🫵
<td nowrap>
Prevents lines within a cell from being broken to fit HTML5 input tag attributes (not all browsers support; visit http://caniuse.com
 for details) 

<input type="email" name=?> 
 Sets a single-line textbox for email addresses 

<input type="url" name=?> 
 Sets a single-line textbox for URLs 
 
<input type="number" name=?> 
 Sets a single-line textbox for a number 

<input type="range" name=?> 
 Sets a single-line text box for a range of numbers 

<input type="date/month/week/time" name=?> 
 Sets a single-line text box with a calendar 
 showing the date/month/week/time 

<input type="search" name=?> 
 Sets a single-line text box for searching 

<input type="color" name=?> 
 Sets a single-line text box for picking a color
</body>
</html>

    <title>A Curated Compendium of HTML Tags</title>
    <style>
        :root {

        }

        }
        .column h2 {

            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        }
        .tag-description { margin: 8px 0; }

            font-weight: bold;
        }

        .status-deprecated { background-color: #f8d7da; color: #842029; }

<body>

    <div class="header">
        <h1>A Curated Compendium of HTML Tags</h1>
        <p>An organized reference for modern web development, based on HTML_Basic_Tags_Cheatsheet original script.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="container">
        <!-- ============================================= -->
        <!-- COLUMN 1: ESSENTIAL & MODERN TAGS             -->
        <!-- ============================================= -->
        <div class="column">
            <h2>Essential & Modern Tags</h2>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;h1&gt;</span> to <span class="tag-name">&lt;h6&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-essential">Essential</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Defines headings. <code>&lt;h1&gt;</code> is the most important, <code>&lt;h6&gt;</code> is the least.</p>
            </div>
            
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;p&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-essential">Essential</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Defines a paragraph.</p>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;a&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-essential">Essential</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Defines a hyperlink. The <code>href</code> attribute specifies the destination URL.</p>
                <div class="example-box"><a href="#">This is a link</a></div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;img&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-essential">Essential</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Embeds an image. Requires <code>src</code> (source) and <code>alt</code> (alternative text) attributes.</p>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;div&gt;</span> and <span class="tag-name">&lt;span&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-essential">Essential</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Generic containers. <code>&lt;div&gt;</code> is a block-level container for layout, and <code>&lt;span&gt;</code> is an inline container for styling text.</p>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;ul&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;ol&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;li&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-essential">Essential</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Used for lists. <code>&lt;ul&gt;</code> for unordered (bulleted) lists, <code>&lt;ol&gt;</code> for ordered (numbered) lists, and <code>&lt;li&gt;</code> for each list item.</p>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;strong&gt;</span> and <span class="tag-name">&lt;em&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-essential">Essential</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Semantic text tags. <code>&lt;strong&gt;</code> indicates importance (bold), and <code>&lt;em&gt;</code> indicates emphasis (italic).</p>
                <div class="example-box"><strong>Important Text</strong> and <em>Emphasized Text</em></div>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;table&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;tr&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;th&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;td&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-essential">Essential for Data</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Used to create tables for tabular data. Not recommended for page layout.</p>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;form&gt;</span> and <span class="tag-name">&lt;input&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-essential">Essential for Forms</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description"><code>&lt;form&gt;</code> contains interactive controls for submitting user data. <code>&lt;input&gt;</code> is the primary form control element.</p>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;header&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;footer&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;main&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;section&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;article&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;aside&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;nav&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">HTML5 semantic elements. They provide meaning to your page structure, which is good for SEO and accessibility.</p>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;audio&gt;</span> and <span class="tag-name">&lt;video&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Embeds sound and video content with native browser controls.</p>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;details&gt;</span> and <span class="tag-name">&lt;summary&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Creates an interactive disclosure widget ("accordion") that the user can open and close.</p>
                <div class="example-box">
                    <details>
                        <summary>Click to see more</summary>
                        <p>This is the hidden content that appears on click.</p>
                    </details>
                </div>
            </div>
            
        </div>

        <!-- ============================================= -->
        <!-- COLUMN 2: DEPRECATED & RARELY USED TAGS       -->
        <!-- ============================================= -->
        <div class="column">
            <h2>Deprecated & Outdated Tags</h2>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;font&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-deprecated">Deprecated</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Used to define font size, color, and face. This is completely replaced by CSS.</p>
                <p class="note">Instead of <code>&lt;font color="blue"&gt;</code>, use CSS: <code>&lt;p style="color: blue;"&gt;</code></p>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;center&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-deprecated">Deprecated</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Used to center text and other content. This is completely replaced by CSS.</p>
                <p class="note">Instead of <code>&lt;center&gt;</code>, use CSS: <code>&lt;div style="text-align: center;"&gt;</code></p>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;b&gt;</span> and <span class="tag-name">&lt;i&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Presentational tags for bold and italic text. While they still work, it's better to use <code>&lt;strong&gt;</code> and <code>&lt;em&gt;</code> which carry semantic meaning.</p>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;strike&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-deprecated">Deprecated</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Defines strikethrough text. The modern equivalent is <code>&lt;s&gt;</code> (for text that is no longer correct) or <code>&lt;del&gt;</code> (for deleted text).</p>
                <div class="example-box"><del>This item is sold out.</del></div>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;acronym&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-deprecated">Deprecated</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Defines an acronym. The modern, preferred tag is <code>&lt;abbr&gt;</code> for all abbreviations.</p>
                <div class="example-box">The <abbr title="World Health Organization">WHO</abbr> was founded in 1948.</div>
            </div>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;frame&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;frameset&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;noframes&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-deprecated">Deprecated</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Used to create framesets, which split the browser window into multiple documents. This approach is outdated and has been replaced by <code>&lt;iframe&gt;</code> and modern CSS layouts.</p>
            </div>
            
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;big&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-deprecated">Deprecated</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Made text one size larger. This should be done with CSS <code>font-size</code>.</p>
            </div>
            
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;tt&gt;</span> <span class="tag-status status-deprecated">Deprecated</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Created "teletype" or monospaced text. The modern equivalent is <code>&lt;code&gt;</code>, <code>&lt;kbd&gt;</code>, or <code>&lt;samp&gt;</code>, or simply using CSS <code>font-family: monospace;</code>.</p>
            </div>
            
        </div>
    </div>

</body>
</html>

<!-- HTML_Basic_Tags_Sheet3.html -->
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>The Ultimate HTML Elements Cheat Sheet</title>
    <style>
        body {
            font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif, "Apple Color Emoji", "Segoe UI Emoji", "Segoe UI Symbol";

        }
        .container {

        }
        h1, h2 {
            border-bottom: 2px solid #dee2e6;
            padding-bottom: 10px;
            color: #212529;

        h1 {
            text-align: center;

            border-bottom-color: #FEC009; /* SCS Accent Color */
        }
        section {
            margin-bottom: 40px;
        }
        .tag-entry {
            margin-bottom: 25px;
            padding: 15px;
            border-left: 4px solid #020169;

        }
        .tag-name {
            font-family: 'Courier New', Courier, monospace;
            background-color: #e9ecef;
            padding: 2px 6px;
            border-radius: 4px;
            font-size: 1.1em;
            color: #d63384;
        }
        .tag-description {
            margin-top: 5px;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
        }
        .tag-example {
            border: 1px dashed #ced4da;
            padding: 15px;
            border-radius: 5px;
            background-color: #fff;
        }
        .code {
            font-family: 'Courier New', Courier, monospace;
            background-color: #e9ecef;
            padding: 2px 4px;
            border-radius: 3px;
            color: #333;
        }
        table {
            width: 100%;
            border-collapse: collapse;
            margin-top: 10px;
        }
        th, td {
            border: 1px solid #dee2e6;
            padding: 8px;
            text-align: left;
        }
        th {
            background-color: #f1f3f5;
        }
        img, video, audio {
            max-width: 100%;
            height: auto;
            display: block;
            margin-top: 10px;
        }

<body>

    <div class="container">
        <h1>The Ultimate HTML Elements Cheat Sheet</h1>

        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <!-- SECTION: DOCUMENT STRUCTURE -->
        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <section id="structure">
            <h2>Document Structure</h2>
            <p>These tags define the overall structure and metadata of an HTML page.</p>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;html&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">The root element of an HTML page. All other elements are descendants of this tag.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;head&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Contains meta-information about the document, like the title, styles, and scripts. This content is not displayed on the page.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;body&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Contains the visible content of the webpage, such as text, images, links, and forms.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;title&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Sets the title of the browser tab or window. It's placed inside the <code class="code">&lt;head&gt;</code>.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;link&gt;</span> and <span class="tag-name">&lt;style&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description"><code class="code">&lt;link&gt;</code> connects to external resources like CSS stylesheets. <code class="code">&lt;style&gt;</code> is used to write CSS directly inside the HTML file (inline/embedded styles).</p>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;script&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Used to embed or link to JavaScript code to add interactivity to the page.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;meta&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Provides metadata such as character set (<code class="code">charset="UTF-8"</code>) or viewport settings for mobile responsiveness.</p>
            </div>
        </section>

        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <!-- SECTION: TEXT AND HEADINGS -->
        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <section id="text">
            <h2>Text and Headings</h2>
            <p>Tags used to structure and format text content.</p>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;h1&gt;</span> to <span class="tag-name">&lt;h6&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Heading tags, from the most important (<code class="code">&lt;h1&gt;</code>) to the least important (<code class="code">&lt;h6&gt;</code>).</p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <h1>This is a Heading 1</h1>
                    <h2>This is a Heading 2</h2>
                    <h3>This is a Heading 3</h3>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;p&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Defines a paragraph of text.</p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <p>This is a paragraph. It's used for standard blocks of text.</p>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;strong&gt;</span> and <span class="tag-name">&lt;b&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description"><code class="code">&lt;strong&gt;</code> indicates important text (semantically strong). <code class="code">&lt;b&gt;</code> simply makes text bold without adding importance.</p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <p>This is <strong>important</strong> text. This is just <b>bold</b> text.</p>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;em&gt;</span> and <span class="tag-name">&lt;i&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description"><code class="code">&lt;em&gt;</code> adds emphasis (semantically). <code class="code">&lt;i&gt;</code> simply italicizes text (e.g., for a technical term).</p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <p>You should <em>really</em> learn this. This is an <i>italicized</i> term.</p>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;a&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">The anchor tag, used to create hyperlinks. The <code class="code">href</code> attribute specifies the destination URL.</p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <a href="https://www.google.com" target="_blank">Click here to visit Google</a>
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>

        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <!-- SECTION: LISTS -->
        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <section id="lists">
            <h2>Lists</h2>
            <p>Tags for creating ordered and unordered lists.</p>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;ul&gt;</span> and <span class="tag-name">&lt;li&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Creates an unordered list (bullet points). Each item in the list is defined by a <code class="code">&lt;li&gt;</code> (list item) tag.</p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <ul>
                        <li>First item</li>
                        <li>Second item</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;ol&gt;</span> and <span class="tag-name">&lt;li&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Creates an ordered list (numbered). Each item is also defined by a <code class="code">&lt;li&gt;</code> tag.</p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <ol>
                        <li>First step</li>
                        <li>Second step</li>
                    </ol>
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>

        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <!-- SECTION: CONTAINERS & LAYOUT -->
        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <section id="layout">
            <h2>Containers & Layout</h2>
            <p>Generic container tags used for grouping content and structuring the page layout.</p>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;div&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">A block-level container. It takes up the full width available and is used to group large sections of content.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;span&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">An inline-level container. It only takes up as much width as necessary and is used to group small pieces of content within a line, like a single word.</p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <p>This is a paragraph with a <span style="color: #020169; font-weight: bold;">styled span</span> inside it.</p>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;header&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;footer&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;main&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;section&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Semantic layout tags that describe their content. They function like <code class="code">&lt;div&gt;</code>s but provide more meaning to the browser and developers.</p>
            </div>
        </section>

        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <!-- SECTION: FORMS -->
        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <section id="forms">
            <h2>Forms</h2>
            <p>Tags used to collect user input.</p>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;form&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">The main container for all form elements.</p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <form action="#">
                        <label for="username">Username:</label>
                        <input type="text" id="username" name="username" placeholder="Enter your username">
                        
                        <label for="password">Password:</label>
                        <input type="password" id="password" name="password">

                        <label>Choose one:</label>
                        <input type="radio" id="option1" name="choice" value="1">
                        <label for="option1">Option 1</label>
                        <input type="radio" id="option2" name="choice" value="2">
                        <label for="option2">Option 2</label>

                        <label for="comments">Comments:</label>
                        <textarea id="comments" name="comments" rows="3"></textarea>

                        <button type="submit">Submit</button>
                    </form>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;input&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">The most versatile form element. The <code class="code">type</code> attribute changes its function (e.g., <code class="code">text</code>, <code class="code">password</code>, <code class="code">checkbox</code>, <code class="code">radio</code>, <code class="code">submit</code>).</p>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;label&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">A caption for an <code class="code">&lt;input&gt;</code> element. The <code class="code">for</code> attribute links it to the input's <code class="code">id</code>, making it clickable.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;textarea&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">A multi-line text input field.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;select&gt;</span> and <span class="tag-name">&lt;option&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Creates a dropdown list. Each choice is defined by an <code class="code">&lt;option&gt;</code> tag.</p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <select name="role">
                        <option value="student">Student</option>
                        <option value="teacher">Teacher</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;button&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">A clickable button, often used to submit a form.</p>
            </div>
        </section>

        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <!-- SECTION: TABLES -->
        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <section id="tables">
            <h2>Tables</h2>
            <p>Tags for displaying data in a tabular format.</p>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;table&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;tr&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;th&gt;</span>, <span class="tag-name">&lt;td&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">
                    <code class="code">&lt;table&gt;</code>: The main table container.<br>
                    <code class="code">&lt;tr&gt;</code>: Table Row. Defines a row of cells.<br>
                    <code class="code">&lt;th&gt;</code>: Table Header. A special cell for column headings (bold and centered).<br>
                    <code class="code">&lt;td&gt;</code>: Table Data. A standard data cell.
                </p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <table>
                        <tr>
                            <th>First Name</th>
                            <th>Last Name</th>
                        </tr>
                        <tr>
                            <td>John</td>
                            <td>Doe</td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr>
                            <td>Jane</td>
                            <td>Smith</td>
                        </tr>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>

        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <!-- SECTION: MEDIA -->
        <!-- ====================================================== -->
        <section id="media">
            <h2>Media</h2>
            <p>Tags for embedding images, audio, and video.</p>

            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;img&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Embeds an image. The <code class="code">src</code> attribute specifies the image path, and the <code class="code">alt</code> attribute provides alternative text for accessibility.</p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <!-- Note: This is a placeholder image. Replace with a real URL to see it. -->
                    <img src="https://via.placeholder.com/300x150" alt="A placeholder image">
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;video&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Embeds a video. The <code class="code">controls</code> attribute adds play/pause buttons. The <code class="code">&lt;source&gt;</code> tag specifies the video file.</p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <!-- This will not play as the source is a placeholder. -->
                    <video controls width="300">
                        <source src="/media/my-video.mp4" type="video/mp4">
                        Your browser does not support the video tag.
                    </video>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="tag-entry">
                <p><span class="tag-name">&lt;audio&gt;</span></p>
                <p class="tag-description">Embeds audio content. Works similarly to the video tag.</p>
                <div class="tag-example">
                    <!-- This will not play as the source is a placeholder. -->
                    <audio controls>
                        <source src="/media/my-audio.mp3" type="audio/mpeg">
                        Your browser does not support the audio element.
                    </audio>
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>

    <h1>Wi-Fi QR Code Generator</h1>

        <input type="text" id="ssid" required><br><br>
        <label for="password">Password:</label>
        <input type="password" id="password" required><br><br>
        <label for="encryption">Encryption Type:</label>
        <select id="encryption">
            <option value="WPA">WPA</option>
            <option value="WPA2">WPA2</option>

        <button id="generate-btn">Generate QR Code</button>

    <script>
        const form = document.getElementById('wifi-form');
        const qrcodeContainer = document.getElementById('qrcode');

        form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const ssid = document.getElementById('ssid').value;
            const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
            const encryption = document.getElementById('encryption').value;
            const qrCodeData = `WIFI:S:${ssid};T:${encryption};P:${password};;`;
            generateQRCode(qrCodeData);
        });

        function generateQRCode(data) {
            qrcodeContainer.innerHTML = '';
            const qrcode = new QRCode(qrcodeContainer, {
                text: data,
                width: 200,
                height: 200,
                colorDark: '#000',
                colorLight: '#fff',
                correctLevel: QRCode.CorrectLevel.H
            });
        }
    </script>
</body>

    <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        // --- UI ELEMENTS ---
        const hamburgerIcon = document.getElementById('hamburger-icon');
        const offCanvasMenu = document.getElementById('off-canvas-menu');
        const profileDisplayIcon = document.getElementById('user-image-display');
        const logoutButton = document.getElementById('logout-button');
        const dashboardLink = document.getElementById('dashboard-link');
        const displayUsernameSpan = document.getElementById('display-username');

        // --- HAMBURGER MENU TOGGLE ---
        hamburgerIcon.addEventListener('click', () => offCanvasMenu.classList.toggle('open'));

        // --- SMART NAVIGATION LOGIC ---
        const redirectToRoleDashboard = () => {
            const userRole = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserRole');
            let url = 'dashboard.html';
            if (userRole) {
                const r = userRole.toLowerCase();
                if (r === 'admin') url = 'dashboard-admin.html';
                else if (r === 'teacher') url = 'dashboard-teacher.html';
                else if (r === 'student') url = 'dashboard-student.html';
                else if (r === 'parent') url = 'dashboard-parents.html';
            }
            window.location.href = url;
        };
        dashboardLink.addEventListener('click', redirectToRoleDashboard);

        // --- LOGOUT ---
        logoutButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
            localStorage.clear();
            window.location.href = 'login.html';
        });

        // --- CORRECTED: Personalize Page and Set Profile Icon ---
        const loggedInUsername = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUsername');
        const loggedInUserId = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserId');

        // 1. Set the welcome username immediately from localStorage (fast)
        if (loggedInUsername) {
            displayUsernameSpan.textContent = loggedInUsername;
        }

        // 2. Fetch full user data to get the profile picture (slower)
    // ... (rest of the code)

    fetch('../data/users.json')
        .then(response => {
            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
            }
            return response.json();
        })
        .then(users => {
            const tempUsers = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tempUsers')) || [];
            const allUsers = [...users, ...tempUsers];
            const currentUser = allUsers.find(user => user.id == loggedInUserId);
            
            if (currentUser) {
                const profilePicPath = currentUser.profilePicture ? currentUser.profilePicture : '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
                profileDisplayIcon.src = profilePicPath;
            } else {
                console.log('User not found');
                profileDisplayIcon.src = '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error loading profile data:', error);
            profileDisplayIcon.src = '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
        });
        
        // Make the top-right profile icon clickable
        profileDisplayIcon.addEventListener('click', () => {
            window.location.href = 'profile.html';
        });

        // Add event listeners for other menu links that were missing IDs
        document.querySelectorAll('.menu-list .menu-item').forEach(item => {
            const span = item.querySelector('span');
            if (!span) return;
            const text = span.textContent.toLowerCase();
            if (text.includes('test library')) item.addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'test-library.html');
            if (text.includes('take an exam')) item.addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'exam-library.html');
            if (text.includes('my profile')) item.addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'profile.html');
            if (text.includes('classes')) item.addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'classes.html');
        });
      
    });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

            td:nth-of-type(1):before { content: "ID:"; }
            td:nth-of-type(2):before { content: "Name:"; }

        /* Animation for form elements */

<body>
    <div class="container">
        <div class="header">
            <h1><i class="fas fa-users-cog"></i> SCS User Manager</h1>
            <p>Administrative Tool for User Management</p>
        </div>

        <div class="alert alert-warning">
            .
            <i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> 
            <strong>Important:</strong> This tool modifies your live <code>users.json</code> file. Use with caution.
        </div>

        <div class="card">
            <h2 id="form-title"><i class="fas fa-user-plus"></i> Add New User</h2>
            <form id="user-form">
                <input type="hidden" id="user-id">
                
                <div class="form-grid">
                    <div class="form-group">
                        <label for="name"><i class="fas fa-user"></i> Full Name</label>
                        <input type="text" id="name" required placeholder="Enter full name">
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="form-group">
                        <label for="username"><i class="fas fa-at"></i> Username</label>
                        <input type="text" id="username" required placeholder="Enter username">
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="form-group">
                        <label for="password">
                            .<i class="fas fa-key"></i> Password</label>
                        <input type="text" id="password" required placeholder="Enter password">
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="form-group">
                        <label for="role"><i class="fas fa-user-tag"></i> Role</label>
                        <select id="role" required>
                            <option value="" disabled selected>Select a role</option>
                            <option value="Student">Student</option>
                            <option value="Teacher">Teacher</option>
                            <option value="Parent">Parent</option>
                            <option value="Admin">Admin</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="form-group">
                        <label for="gender"><i class="fas fa-venus-mars"></i> Gender</label>
                        <select id="gender" required>
                            <option value="" disabled selected>Select gender</option>
                            <option value="Male">Male</option>
                            <option value="Female">Female</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="form-group" id="class-selection" style="display: none;">
                        <label for="classid"><i class="fas fa-graduation-cap"></i> Class</label>
                        <select id="classid">
                            <option value="" disabled selected>Select a class</option>
                            <option value="JS1">JS1 (Basic Seven Lavender)</option>
                            <option value="JS2">JS2 (Basic Eight Tulip)</option>
                            <option value="JS3">JS3 (Basic Nine Sunflower)</option>
                            <option value="SS1">SS1 (Senior Class One)</option>
                            <option value="SS2">SS2 (Senior Class Two)</option>
                            <option value="SS3">SS3 (Senior Class Three)</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="form-group" id="class-input" style="display: none;">
                        <label for="class"><i class="fas fa-graduation-cap"></i> Class/Department</label>
                        <input type="text" id="class" placeholder="Enter class or department">
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="form-group">
                        <label for="email"><i class="fas fa-envelope"></i> Email</label>
                        <input type="email" id="email" placeholder="Enter email address">
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="form-group">
                        <label for="date-of-birth"><i class="fas fa-birthday-cake"></i> Date of Birth</label>
                        <input type="date" id="date-of-birth" required>
                    </div>
                </div>
                
                <div class="action-buttons">
                    <button type="submit" id="submit-btn" class="btn btn-primary">
                        <i class="fas fa-plus-circle"></i> Add User
                    </button>
                    <button type="button" id="cancel-edit-btn" class="btn btn-secondary" style="display: none;">
                        <i class="fas fa-times"></i> Cancel Edit
                    </button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>

        <div class="card">
            <h2><i class="fas fa-list"></i> Registered Users</h2>
            <div class="table-container">
                <table id="users-table">
                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th>ID</th>
                            <th>Name</th>
                            <th>Username</th>
                            <th>Role</th>
                            <th>Class</th>
                            <th>Gender</th>
                            <th>Email</th>
                            <th>Passport</th>
                            <th>Actions</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody><!-- User data will be loaded here --></tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="card json-output-container">
            <h2><i class="fas fa-code"></i> Updated JSON Output</h2>
            
            <div class="instructions">
                <p><strong>Instructions:</strong></p>
                <ol>
                    <li>Copy the JSON content below</li>
                    <li>Open <code>data/users.json</code> in your project</li>
                    <li>Replace the content with this updated JSON</li>
                    <li>Save the file and upload to your server/GitHub</li>
                </ol>
            </div>
            
            <textarea id="json-output" readonly></textarea>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        /**
         * SCS User Manager - Admin Tool
         * 
         * This script provides functionality for managing users in the SCS system.
         * It allows administrators to add, edit, and delete users, and generates
         * the updated JSON for the users.json file.
         */

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            // DOM Element References
            const tableBody = document.querySelector('#users-table tbody');
            const userForm = document.getElementById('user-form');
            const jsonOutput = document.getElementById('json-output');
            const formTitle = document.getElementById('form-title');
            const submitBtn = document.getElementById('submit-btn');
            const cancelEditBtn = document.getElementById('cancel-edit-btn');
            const userIdInput = document.getElementById('user-id');
            const nameInput = document.getElementById('name');
            const usernameInput = document.getElementById('username');
            const passwordInput = document.getElementById('password');
            const roleInput = document.getElementById('role');
            const genderInput = document.getElementById('gender');
            const classidInput = document.getElementById('classid');
            const classInput = document.getElementById('class');
            const emailInput = document.getElementById('email');
            const dateOfBirthInput = document.getElementById('date-of-birth');

            // Application State
            let users = [];

            /**
             * Loads users from the users.json file
             * @async
             */
            const loadUsers = async () => {
                try {
                    const response = await fetch('../data/users.json');
                    if (!response.ok) throw new Error('File not found. Make sure users.json is in the /data folder.');
                    users = await response.json();
                    renderTable();
                    updateJsonOutput();
                } catch (error) {
                    showError(`Error loading users.json: ${error.message}`);
                }
            };

            /**
             * Renders the user table with current user data
             */
            const renderTable = () => {
                tableBody.innerHTML = '';
                
                if (users.length === 0) {
                    const row = tableBody.insertRow();
                    row.innerHTML = `<td colspan="9" style="text-align: center; padding: 30px; color: var(--text-light);">
                        <i class="fas fa-users" style="font-size: 3rem; margin-bottom: 15px; display: block; opacity: 0.5;"></i>
                        No users found. Add a user to get started.
                    </td>`;
                    return;
                }
                
                users.forEach(user => {
                    const row = tableBody.insertRow();
                    row.innerHTML = `
                        <td>${user.id}</td>
                        <td>${user.name}</td>
                        <td>${user.username}</td>
                        <td><span class="badge">${user.role}</span></td>
                        <td>${user.class || 'N/A'}</td>
                        <td>${user.gender}</td>
                        <td>${user.email || 'N/A'}</td>
                        <td>${user.profilePicture || 'Default'}</td>
                        <td>
                            <button class="btn btn-accent edit-btn" data-id="${user.id}">
                                <i class="fas fa-edit"></i> Edit
                            </button>
                            <button class="btn btn-danger delete-btn" data-id="${user.id}">
                                <i class="fas fa-trash"></i> Delete
                            </button>
                        </td>
                    `;
                });
            };

            /**
             * Updates the JSON output textarea with formatted user data
             */
            const updateJsonOutput = () => {
                const orderedUsers = users.map(user => ({
                    id: user.id,
                    username: user.username,
                    'date of birth': user['date of birth'],
                    password: user.password,
                    name: user.name,
                    role: user.role,
                    class: user.class,
                    classid: user.classid,
                    gender: user.gender,
                    email: user.email,
                    profilePicture: user.profilePicture,
                }));
                jsonOutput.value = JSON.stringify(orderedUsers, null, 2);
            };

            /**
             * Handles form submission for adding or updating users
             * @param {Event} e - The form submission event
             */
            userForm.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                
                const id = userIdInput.value;
                const classid = roleInput.value === 'Student' ? classidInput.value : '';
                const className = roleInput.value === 'Student' ? getClassName(classid) : classInput.value;
                
                const user = {
                    name: nameInput.value,
                    username: usernameInput.value,
                    password: passwordInput.value,
                    role: roleInput.value,
                    classid: classid,
                    class: className,
                    gender: genderInput.value,
                    email: emailInput.value || `${usernameInput.value}@scs.infy.uk`,
                    profilePicture: genderInput.value === 'Male' ? '../assets/profile_picture/male.jpeg' : '../assets/profile_picture/female.jpeg',
                    'date of birth': dateOfBirthInput.value,
                };

                if (id) {
                    // Update existing user
                    const index = users.findIndex(u => u.id === id);
                    users[index] = { ...users[index], ...user };
                    showSuccess('User updated successfully!');
                } else {
                    // Add new user
                    user.id = `user_${Date.now()}`;
                    users.push(user);
                    showSuccess('User added successfully!');
                }
                
                renderTable();
                updateJsonOutput();
                resetForm();
            });

            /**
             * Handles click events on the user table (edit and delete actions)
             * @param {Event} e - The click event
             */
            tableBody.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                const id = e.target.closest('button')?.dataset.id;
                if (!id) return;
                
                if (e.target.closest('.edit-btn')) {
                    const user = users.find(u => u.id === id);
                    populateFormForEditing(user);
                } else if (e.target.closest('.delete-btn')) {
                    deleteUser(id);
                }
            });

            /**
             * Populates the form with user data for editing
             * @param {Object} user - The user object to edit
             */
            const populateFormForEditing = (user) => {
                formTitle.textContent = 'Edit User';
                submitBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-save"></i> Update User';
                submitBtn.className = 'btn btn-accent';
                cancelEditBtn.style.display = 'inline-block';
                
                userIdInput.value = user.id;
                nameInput.value = user.name;
                usernameInput.value = user.username;
                passwordInput.value = user.password;
                roleInput.value = user.role;
                
                if (user.role === 'Student') {
                    classidInput.value = user.classid || '';
                    document.getElementById('class-selection').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('class-input').style.display = 'none';
                } else {
                    classInput.value = user.class || '';
                    document.getElementById('class-selection').style.display = 'none';
                    document.getElementById('class-input').style.display = 'block';
                }
                
                genderInput.value = user.gender;
                emailInput.value = user.email || '';
                dateOfBirthInput.value = user['date of birth'] || '';
                
                // Scroll to form
                userForm.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
            };

            /**
             * Deletes a user after confirmation
             * @param {string} id - The ID of the user to delete
             */
            const deleteUser = (id) => {
                const userName = users.find(u => u.id === id).name;
                
                if (confirm(`Are you sure you want to delete user: ${userName}? This action cannot be undone.`)) {
                    users = users.filter(u => u.id !== id);
                    renderTable();
                    updateJsonOutput();
                    showSuccess('User deleted successfully!');
                }
            };

            /**
             * Handles role selection changes to show appropriate class fields
             */
            roleInput.addEventListener('change', () => {
                if (roleInput.value === 'Student') {
                    document.getElementById('class-selection').style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('class-input').style.display = 'none';
                } else {
                    document.getElementById('class-selection').style.display = 'none';
                    document.getElementById('class-input').style.display = 'block';
                }
            });

            /**
             * Gets the full class name based on the class ID
             * @param {string} classid - The class ID
             * @returns {string} The full class name
             */
            const getClassName = (classid) => {
                const classNames = {
                    'JS1': 'Basic Seven Lavender',
                    'JS2': 'Basic Eight Tulip',
                    'JS3': 'Basic Nine Sunflower',
                    'SS1': 'Senior Class One',
                    'SS2': 'Senior Class Two',
                    'SS3': 'Senior Class Three',
                };
                return classNames[classid] || '';
            };

            /**
             * Resets the form to its initial state
             */
            const resetForm = () => {
                userForm.reset();
                userIdInput.value = '';
                formTitle.textContent = 'Add New User';
                submitBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-plus-circle"></i> Add User';
                submitBtn.className = 'btn btn-primary';
                cancelEditBtn.style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('class-selection').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('class-input').style.display = 'none';
            };

            /**
             * Displays a success message
             * @param {string} message - The success message to display
             */
            const showSuccess = (message) => {
                // Create or update success alert
                let alert = document.getElementById('success-alert');
                if (!alert) {
                    alert = document.createElement('div');
                    alert.id = 'success-alert';
                    alert.className = 'alert';
                    alert.style.backgroundColor = 'rgba(40, 167, 69, 0.2)';
                    alert.style.borderLeft = '4px solid var(--success)';
                    alert.style.color = '#155724';
                    document.querySelector('.container').insertBefore(alert, document.querySelector('.card'));
                }
                
                alert.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> <strong>Success:</strong> ${message}`;
                
                // Auto-hide after 5 seconds
                setTimeout(() => {
                    alert.style.display = 'none';
                }, 5000);
            };

            /**
             * Displays an error message
             * @param {string} message - The error message to display
             */
            const showError = (message) => {
                alert(`Error: ${message}`);
            };

            // Event listener for cancel edit button
            cancelEditBtn.addEventListener('click', resetForm);
            
            // Initialize the application
            loadUsers();
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <div class="page-container">
        <!-- The Title will be loaded here by JavaScript -->

    <script>
        // This event listener ensures the script runs only after the full HTML page has loaded
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            
            // This JavaScript object holds all the content for our page.
            // It acts as a temporary, "mock" database.
            // The content string uses backticks (`) to allow for multiple lines.
            const pageData = {
                title: "St. Christopher School",
                content: `
                    <h2><i class="fas fa-school" style="color: #FFD43B;"></i> Our Mission & Vision</h2>
                    <p>At St. Christopher School, our mission is to foster a vibrant learning community that inspires academic excellence, creativity, and personal growth. We are dedicated to nurturing the next generation of leaders and innovators, guided by our motto: <strong>"Best Our Only Option"</strong>.</p>
                    
                    <p>We believe in a holistic approach to education, combining rigorous academics with robust programs in arts, athletics, and community service. Our dedicated faculty are experts in their fields and are committed to providing a supportive and challenging environment for all students.</p>
                    <div class="admissions-highlight">
                        <h3><i class="fa-solid fa-bullhorn fa-shake"></i> Admissions for 2025/2026 Are Now Open!</h3>
                        <p>We are excited to announce that we are now accepting applications for the upcoming academic session. We welcome students who are curious, motivated, and eager to make a positive impact on the world.</p>
                        <p>To learn more about the application process, key dates, and our curriculum, please feel free to contact our admissions office. We look forward to welcoming you to the St. Christopher family!</p>
                    </div>

                    <h2><i class="fa-solid fa-cogs fa-fade"></i> Our Facilities</h2>
                    <p>Our campus features state-of-the-art facilities, including modern classrooms, fully-equipped science labs, a comprehensive library, and expansive sports grounds to support the all-round development of our students.</p>
                `
            };

            // Get the HTML elements where we want to display our content
            const titleElement = document.getElementById('page-title');
            const contentElement = document.getElementById('page-content');

            // Insert the data from our object into the HTML elements
            // We prepend an icon to the title for a nice visual touch
            titleElement.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-info-circle"></i> ${pageData.title}`;
            
            // Use .innerHTML because our content string contains HTML tags that we want the browser to render
            contentElement.innerHTML = pageData.content; 
        });
    </script>

</body>
</html>

      <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const hamburgerIcon = document.getElementById('hamburger-icon');
    const offCanvasMenu = document.getElementById('off-canvas-menu');
    const profileDisplayIcon = document.getElementById('user-image-display');
    const logoutButton = document.getElementById('logout-button');

    // --- Hamburger Menu Toggle ---
    hamburgerIcon.addEventListener('click', () => {
        offCanvasMenu.classList.toggle('open');
    });

    // --- Smart Navigation Links Setup ---
    const dashboardLink = document.getElementById('dashboard-link');
    const testLibraryLink = document.getElementById('test-library-link');
    const examTakingLink = document.getElementById('exam-library-link');
    const profileLink = document.getElementById('profile-link');
    const classesLink = document.getElementById('classes-link');
    const guideFaqLink = document.getElementById('guide-faq-link');
    const aboutLink = document.getElementById('about-link');
    const contactLink = document.getElementById('contact-link');

    // SMART DASHBOARD LINK
    dashboardLink.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const userRole = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserRole');
        let dashboardUrl = 'dashboard.html'; // Default fallback
        if (userRole) {
            const role = userRole.toLowerCase();
            if (role === 'admin') dashboardUrl = 'dashboard-admin.html';
            else if (role === 'teacher') dashboardUrl = 'dashboard-teacher.html';
            else if (role === 'student') dashboardUrl = 'dashboard-student.html';
            else if (role === 'parent') dashboardUrl = 'dashboard-parents.html';
        }
        window.location.href = dashboardUrl;
    });

    // REGULAR LINKS (These don't need special logic)
    testLibraryLink.addEventListener('click', () => { window.location.href = 'test-library.html'; });
    examTakingLink.addEventListener('click', () => { window.location.href = 'exam-library.html'; });
    profileLink.addEventListener('click', () => { window.location.href = 'profile.html'; });
    classesLink.addEventListener('click', () => { window.location.href = 'classes.html'; });
    guideFaqLink.addEventListener('click', () => { window.location.href = 'guide-FAQ.html'; });
    aboutLink.addEventListener('click', () => { window.location.href = 'about.html'; });
    contactLink.addEventListener('click', () => { window.location.href = 'contact.html'; });

    // --- Authentication Check & Profile Display ---
    const loggedInUserId = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserId');
    if (!loggedInUserId) {
        window.location.href = '../login.html'; // Go up one level to find login.html
        return;
    }

    fetch('../data/users.json')
        .then(response => response.ok ? response.json() : Promise.reject('Failed to load user data'))
        .then(users => {
            const studentData = users.find(user => user.id == loggedInUserId);
            if (profileDisplayIcon && studentData) {
                const profilePicPath = studentData.profilePicture ? studentData.profilePicture : '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
                profileDisplayIcon.src = profilePicPath;
                profileDisplayIcon.alt = `${studentData.name}'s Profile`;
                profileDisplayIcon.onclick = () => window.location.href = 'profile.html';
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error loading student data:', error);
            if (profileDisplayIcon) {
                profileDisplayIcon.src = '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
            }
        });

    // --- Logout Button Functionality ---
    logoutButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
        localStorage.clear(); // Clears all session data
        window.location.href = 'login.html'; // Go up one level to find login.html
    });
});
  </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        // --- UI & NAVIGATION (Same as other pages) ---
        const hamburgerIcon = document.getElementById('hamburger-icon');
        const offCanvasMenu = document.getElementById('off-canvas-menu');
        hamburgerIcon.addEventListener('click', () => offCanvasMenu.classList.toggle('open'));

        const redirectToRoleDashboard = () => {
            const userRole = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserRole');
            let url = 'dashboard.html';
            if (userRole) {
                const r = userRole.toLowerCase();
                if (r === 'admin') url = 'dashboard-admin.html';
                else if (r === 'teacher') url = 'dashboard-teacher.html';
                else if (r === 'student') url = 'dashboard-student.html';
                else if (r === 'parent') url = 'dashboard-parents.html';
            }
            window.location.href = url;
        };
        document.getElementById('dashboard-link').addEventListener('click', redirectToRoleDashboard);
        document.getElementById('test-library-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'test-library.html');
        document.getElementById('exam-library-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'exam-library.html');
        document.getElementById('profile-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'profile.html');
        document.getElementById('classes-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'classes.html');
        document.getElementById('contact-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'contact.html');
        document.getElementById('logout-button').addEventListener('click', () => {
            localStorage.clear();
            window.location.href = 'login.html';
        });

        // --- PROFILE ICON ---
        const profileDisplayIcon = document.getElementById('user-image-display');
        const loggedInUserId = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserId');
        if (loggedInUserId) {
                    // 2. Fetch full user data to get the profile picture (slower)
    // ... (rest of the code)

    fetch('../data/users.json')
        .then(response => {
            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
            }
            return response.json();
        })
        .then(users => {
            const tempUsers = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tempUsers')) || [];
            const allUsers = [...users, ...tempUsers];
            const currentUser = allUsers.find(user => user.id == loggedInUserId);
            
            if (currentUser) {
                const profilePicPath = currentUser.profilePicture ? currentUser.profilePicture : '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
                profileDisplayIcon.src = profilePicPath;
            } else {
                console.log('User not found');
                profileDisplayIcon.src = '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error loading profile data:', error);
            profileDisplayIcon.src = '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
        });
        
        }
        profileDisplayIcon.addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'profile.html');

        // --- CONTACT FORM SUBMISSION ---
        const contactForm = document.getElementById('contact-form');
        contactForm.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault(); // Prevent the form from actually submitting
            
            // In a real application, you would send this data to a server.
            // For this static site, we will simulate a success message.
            alert('Thank you for your message! We will get back to you shortly.');
            
            contactForm.reset(); // Clear the form fields
        });
    });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

  <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      // DOM Elements
      const menuToggle = document.getElementById('menu-toggle');
      const closeSidebar = document.getElementById('close-sidebar');
      const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
      const overlay = document.getElementById('overlay');
      const userProfileImg = document.getElementById('user-profile-img');
      
      // Menu Toggle Functionality
      menuToggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
        sidebar.classList.add('open');
        overlay.classList.add('active');
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      });
      
      closeSidebar.addEventListener('click', closeMenu);
      overlay.addEventListener('click', closeMenu);
      
      function closeMenu() {
        sidebar.classList.remove('open');
        overlay.classList.remove('active');
        document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
      }
      
      // Navigation Links
      const setupNavigation = () => {
        const dashboardLink = document.getElementById('dashboard-link');
        const testLibraryLink = document.getElementById('test-library-link');
        const examTakingLink = document.getElementById('exam-library-link');
        const profileLink = document.getElementById('profile-link');
        const classesLink = document.getElementById('classes-link');
        const guideFaqLink = document.getElementById('guide-faq-link');
        const aboutLink = document.getElementById('about-link');
        const contactLink = document.getElementById('contact-link');
        const gamehallLink = document.getElementById('gamehall-link');
        const logoutButton = document.getElementById('logout-button');
        
        // SMART DASHBOARD LINK
        dashboardLink.addEventListener('click', () => {
          closeMenu();
          const userRole = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserRole');
          let dashboardUrl = 'dashboard.html';
          if (userRole) {
            const role = userRole.toLowerCase();
            if (role === 'admin') dashboardUrl = 'dashboard-admin.html';
            else if (role === 'teacher') dashboardUrl = 'dashboard-teacher.html';
            else if (role === 'student') dashboardUrl = 'dashboard-student.html';
            else if (role === 'parent') dashboardUrl = 'dashboard-parents.html';
          }
          window.location.href = dashboardUrl;
        });
        
        // REGULAR LINKS
        testLibraryLink.addEventListener('click', () => { closeMenu(); window.location.href = 'test-library.html'; });
        examTakingLink.addEventListener('click', () => { closeMenu(); window.location.href = 'exam-library.html'; });
        profileLink.addEventListener('click', () => { closeMenu(); window.location.href = 'profile.html'; });
        classesLink.addEventListener('click', () => { closeMenu(); window.location.href = 'classes.html'; });
        guideFaqLink.addEventListener('click', () => { closeMenu(); window.location.href = 'guide-FAQ.html'; });
        aboutLink.addEventListener('click', () => { closeMenu(); window.location.href = 'about.html'; });
        contactLink.addEventListener('click', () => { closeMenu(); window.location.href = 'contact.html'; });
        gamehallLink.addEventListener('click', () => { closeMenu(); window.location.href = 'gamehall.html'; });
        

// --- Authentication Check & Profile Display ---
const loggedInUserId = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserId');
let studentData = {}; 

if (!loggedInUserId) {
    // If not logged in, redirect to login page
    window.location.href = 'login.html';
    return; 
}

// Get the user profile image element
const userProfileImg = document.getElementById('user-profile-img');

fetch('../data/users.json')
    .then(response => {
        if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
        }
        return response.json();
    })
    .then(data => {
        if (Array.isArray(data)) {
            studentData = data.find(user => user.id == loggedInUserId);
            if (!studentData) {
                console.warn(`User with ID ${loggedInUserId} not found in users.json. Using generic student data.`);
                studentData = { id: loggedInUserId, name: 'Generic Student', username: 'generic.user', 'date of birth': 'Unknown', role: 'Student' };
            }
        } else {
            studentData = data;
        }

        if (userProfileImg && studentData) {
            const profilePicPath = studentData.profilePicture && studentData.profilePicture !== '' 
                                   ? studentData.profilePicture 
                                   : '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
            userProfileImg.src = profilePicPath;
            userProfileImg.alt = `${studentData.name}'s Profile Picture`;
            userProfileImg.title = `Go to ${studentData.name}'s Profile`;
            userProfileImg.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            userProfileImg.onclick = () => {
                window.location.href = 'profile.html';
            };
        }
    })
    .catch(error => {
        console.error('Error loading student data:', error);
        if (userProfileImg) {
            userProfileImg.src = '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
            userProfileImg.alt = 'Default Profile Picture';
            userProfileImg.title = 'Default Profile';
            userProfileImg.onclick = null;
        }
    });

// --- Logout Button Functionality ---
logoutButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
    localStorage.clear(); // Clears all session data
    window.location.href = 'login.html'; // Redirect to login page
    
    });
  }
});

  </script>
</body>
</html>
