<body>
    <div class="hamburger-icon" id="hamburger-icon"><i class="fas fa-bars"></i></div>
    <nav class="off-canvas-menu" id="off-canvas-menu">
        <ul class="menu-list">
            <li class="menu-item" id="dashboard-link"><i class="fas fa-tachometer-alt"></i><span>Dashboard</span></li>
            <li class="menu-item" id="exam-library-link"><i class="fas fa-pencil-alt"></i><span>Exam Library</span></li>
            <li class="menu-item" id="profile-link"><i class="fas fa-user-tie"></i><span>My Profile</span></li>
            <li class="menu-item" id="logout-button"><i class="fas fa-sign-out-alt"></i><span>Logout</span></li>
        </ul>
    </nav>

    <main>
        <div class="main-container">
            <h1 id="exam-title">Loading Exam...</h1>
            
            <div id="exam-questions-wrapper">
                <form id="test-form"></form>
                <div id="question-navigation-panel" class="question-nav-panel"></div>
                <div class="navigation-buttons">
                    <button id="prev-question-button" disabled>Previous</button>
                    <button id="next-question-button">Next</button>
                </div>
                <div class="finish-button-container">
                    <button id="finish-exam-button">Finish Exam</button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div id="submission-summary-wrapper" style="display: none;">
                <div class="submission-summary-container">
                    <h2>Exam Summary</h2>
                    <p>Review the status of your answers. Click "Submit Now" to finalize and grade your exam.</p>
                    <div id="summary-table-container"></div>
                    <div class="summary-actions">
                        <button id="go-back-to-exam-btn">Return to Exam</button>
                        <button id="final-submit-btn">Submit Now</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
            
            <div id="results-wrapper" style="display: none;"></div>
        </div>
    </main>
  
    <img id="user-image-display" src="../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg" alt="User Profile" class="profile-display-icon">
    <footer>&copy; 2024 TestHall</footer>

    <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        // --- SECTION 1: AUTHENTICATION GUARD ---
        const loggedInUserId = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserId');
        const expirationTime = localStorage.getItem('sessionExpiration');
        if (!loggedInUserId) {
            window.location.href = '../login.html';
            return;
        }
        if (expirationTime && Date.now() > parseInt(expirationTime, 10)) {
            localStorage.clear();
            alert('Your temporary 60-minute session has expired.');
            window.location.href = 'login.html?reason=unregistered';
            return;
        }

        // --- SECTION 2: ELEMENT REFERENCES & STATE ---
        const ui = {
            title: document.getElementById('exam-title'),
            examWrapper: document.getElementById('exam-questions-wrapper'),
            summaryWrapper: document.getElementById('submission-summary-wrapper'),
            resultsWrapper: document.getElementById('results-wrapper'),
            form: document.getElementById('test-form'),
            navPanel: document.getElementById('question-navigation-panel'),
            prevBtn: document.getElementById('prev-question-button'),
            nextBtn: document.getElementById('next-question-button'),
            finishBtn: document.getElementById('finish-exam-button'),
            goBackBtn: document.getElementById('go-back-to-exam-btn'),
            submitBtn: document.getElementById('final-submit-btn'),
            hamburger: document.getElementById('hamburger-icon'),
            menu: document.getElementById('off-canvas-menu'),
            profileIcon: document.getElementById('user-image-display'),
            summaryTableContainer: document.getElementById('summary-table-container')
        };
        
        let state = {
            questions: [], 
            userAnswers: [], 
            studentData: {}, 
            testData: {},
            score: 0, 
            currentIndex: 0
        };

        // --- SECTION 3: INITIALIZATION ---
        const initializeExam = async () => {
            try {
                const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
                const testFileName = urlParams.get('test');
                if (!testFileName) throw new Error("No exam file specified.");
                
                // Try to get user data from localStorage first
                const loggedInUserString = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUser');
                if (loggedInUserString) {
                    state.studentData = JSON.parse(loggedInUserString);
                } else {
                    // Fallback to fetching from users.json
                    const usersResponse = await fetch('../data/users.json');
                    if (!usersResponse.ok) throw new Error("Could not load user data.");
                    
                    const users = await usersResponse.json();
                    const tempUsers = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tempUsers')) || [];
                    const allUsers = [...users, ...tempUsers];
                    
                    // Try to find user by ID
                    state.studentData = allUsers.find(user => 
                        user.id && user.id.toString() === loggedInUserId.toString()
                    );
                    
                    // If still not found, create minimal user object
                    if (!state.studentData) {
                        state.studentData = {
                            id: loggedInUserId,
                            name: localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserName') || 'Student',
                            class: 'Unknown'
                        };
                    }
                }
                
                // Load exam data
                const examResponse = await fetch(`../data/${testFileName}.json`);
                if (!examResponse.ok) throw new Error("Could not load exam file.");
                state.testData = await examResponse.json();
                
                state.questions = state.testData.questions || [];
                state.userAnswers = new Array(state.questions.length).fill(null);
                ui.title.textContent = state.testData.testName || 'Exam Taking';
                
                // Set profile picture if available
                if (state.studentData.profilePicture) {
                    ui.profileIcon.src = `../${state.studentData.profilePicture}`;
                }
                
                setupUI();
                generateQuestionNavPanel();
                displayQuestion(0);

            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Initialization Failed:", error);
                alert(`Error: ${error.message}. Redirecting...`);
                window.location.href = 'exam-library.html';
            }
        };
        
        // --- SECTION 4: UI SETUP AND EVENT LISTENERS ---
        const setupUI = () => {
            ui.hamburger.addEventListener('click', () => ui.menu.classList.toggle('open'));
            
            ui.profileIcon.addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'profile.html');
            
            const redirectToRoleDashboard = () => {
                const role = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserRole') || state.studentData.role || 'student';
                let url = 'dashboard.html';
                if (role.toLowerCase() === 'admin') url = 'dashboard-admin.html';
                else if (role.toLowerCase() === 'teacher') url = 'dashboard-teacher.html';
                else if (role.toLowerCase() === 'student') url = 'dashboard-student.html';
                else if (role.toLowerCase() === 'parent') url = 'dashboard-parents.html';
                window.location.href = url;
            };
            
            document.getElementById('dashboard-link').addEventListener('click', redirectToRoleDashboard);
            document.getElementById('exam-library-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'exam-library.html');
            document.getElementById('profile-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'profile.html');
            
            document.getElementById('logout-button').addEventListener('click', () => {
                localStorage.clear();
                window.location.href = '../login.html';
            });
            
            ui.prevBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { 
                if (state.currentIndex > 0) { 
                    saveCurrentAnswer(); 
                    displayQuestion(state.currentIndex - 1); 
                } 
            });
            
            ui.nextBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { 
                if (state.currentIndex < state.questions.length - 1) { 
                    saveCurrentAnswer(); 
                    displayQuestion(state.currentIndex + 1); 
                } 
            });
            
            ui.finishBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { 
                saveCurrentAnswer(); 
                showSubmissionSummary(); 
            });
            
            ui.goBackBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { 
                ui.summaryWrapper.style.display = 'none'; 
                ui.examWrapper.style.display = 'block'; 
                ui.title.textContent = state.testData.testName; 
            });
            
            ui.submitBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                ui.summaryWrapper.style.display = 'none';
                ui.resultsWrapper.style.display = 'block';
                ui.title.textContent = "Final Result";
                calculateResults();
                setTimeout(() => alert("Your results have been successfully submitted."), 1000);
            });
        };
        
        // --- SECTION 5: DYNAMIC UI FUNCTIONS ---
        const displayQuestion = (index) => {
            state.currentIndex = index;
            const q = state.questions[index];
            
            // FIXED: Proper image handling
            let imageHTML = '';
            if (q.imageURL && q.imageURL.trim() !== "") {
                // Use the imageURL as provided in the JSON
                imageHTML = `<img src="${q.imageURL}" alt="Question Diagram" class="question-image">`;
            }
            
            const optionsHTML = q.options.map((opt, i) => 
                `<li>
                    <label>
                        <input type="radio" name="q${index}" value="${opt}" ${state.userAnswers[index] === opt ? 'checked' : ''}> 
                        ${String.fromCharCode(65+i)}. ${opt}
                    </label>
                </li>`
            ).join('');
            
            ui.form.innerHTML = `
                <div class="question-container">
                    <p><strong>Question ${index + 1}:</strong> ${q.question}</p> 
                    ${imageHTML} 
                    <ul>${optionsHTML}</ul>
                    <div class="question-actions">
                        <button type="button" class="clear-answer-btn">Clear My Answer</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
            
            updateNavigationButtons();
            updateQuestionNavPanel();
            
            ui.form.querySelector('.clear-answer-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
                state.userAnswers[state.currentIndex] = null;
                displayQuestion(state.currentIndex);
            });
        };
        
        const updateNavigationButtons = () => {
            ui.prevBtn.disabled = state.currentIndex === 0;
            ui.nextBtn.disabled = state.currentIndex === state.questions.length - 1;
        };
        
        const generateQuestionNavPanel = () => {
            ui.navPanel.innerHTML = state.questions.map((_, i) => 
                `<button class="nav-question-btn">${i + 1}</button>`
            ).join('');
            
            ui.navPanel.querySelectorAll('.nav-question-btn').forEach((btn, i) => {
                btn.addEventListener('click', () => { 
                    saveCurrentAnswer(); 
                    displayQuestion(i); 
                });
            });
        };
        
        const updateQuestionNavPanel = () => {
            ui.navPanel.querySelectorAll('.nav-question-btn').forEach((btn, i) => {
                btn.classList.remove('current', 'answered');
                if (state.userAnswers[i] !== null) btn.classList.add('answered');
                if (i === state.currentIndex) btn.classList.add('current');
            });
        };
        
        const saveCurrentAnswer = () => {
            const selected = ui.form.querySelector(`input[name="q${state.currentIndex}"]:checked`);
            state.userAnswers[state.currentIndex] = selected ? selected.value : null;
        };

        // --- SECTION 6: WORKFLOW & RESULTS FUNCTIONS ---
        const showSubmissionSummary = () => {
            ui.examWrapper.style.display = 'none';
            ui.summaryWrapper.style.display = 'block';
            ui.title.textContent = 'Exam Submission Summary';
            
            const tableHTML = `
                <table class="summary-table">
                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th>Question</th>
                            <th>Status</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        ${state.userAnswers.map((ans, i) => `
                            <tr>
                                <td>${i + 1}</td>
                                <td>
                                    ${ans !== null 
                                        ? '<span class="status-answered">ANSWERED</span>' 
                                        : '<span class="status-unanswered">NOT ANSWERED</span>'}
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        `).join('')}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            `;
            
            ui.summaryTableContainer.innerHTML = tableHTML;
        };

        const calculateResults = () => {
            state.score = state.userAnswers.reduce((acc, ans, i) => 
                acc + (ans === state.questions[i].correctAnswer ? 1 : 0), 0);
            
            const percentage = state.questions.length > 0 
                ? Math.round((state.score / state.questions.length) * 100) 
                : 0;

            // FIX: Get the correct profile picture path
            const profilePicPath = state.studentData.profilePicture 
                ? (state.studentData.profilePicture.startsWith('../') 
                    ? state.studentData.profilePicture 
                    : `../${state.studentData.profilePicture}`)
                : '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';

            ui.resultsWrapper.innerHTML = `
                <div class="detailed-results-preview">
                    <section class="header-section">
                        <div><img src="../assets/images/logo.png" alt="School Logo" style="max-width:80px;"></div>
                        <div><h2>St. Christopher School</h2><p style="font-weight: bold;">EXAMINATION RESULT</p></div>
                    </section>
                    <section>
                        <h2>Candidate & Exam Information</h2>
                        <div class="student-info">
                            <div>
                                <!-- FIX: Access user data correctly -->
                                <p><strong>Name:</strong> ${state.studentData.name || state.studentData.username || 'N/A'}</p>
                                <p><strong>Class:</strong> ${state.studentData.class || state.studentData.grade || 'N/A'}</p>
                                <p><strong>Subject:</strong> ${state.testData.testName || 'N/A'}</p>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <!-- FIX: Use the corrected profile picture path -->
                                <img src="${profilePicPath}" 
                                    alt="Profile" class="profile-pic-preview">
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </section>
                    <section class="summary-section">
                        <h2>Performance Summary</h2>
                        <p>Score: ${state.score} / ${state.questions.length}</p>
                        <p>Percentage: ${percentage}%</p>
                    </section>
                    <section class="action-buttons-container">
                        <button id="download-pdf-button" class="download-pdf-button">Download Detailed PDF</button>
                        <a id="dashboard-redirect-link" href="#" class="dashboard-link-button">Back to Dashboard</a>
                    </section>
                </div>
            `;
            
            document.getElementById('download-pdf-button').addEventListener('click', generateDetailedResultsPDF);
            document.getElementById('dashboard-redirect-link').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                const role = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserRole') || state.studentData.role || 'student';
                let url = 'dashboard.html';
                if (role.toLowerCase() === 'admin') url = 'dashboard-admin.html';
                else if (role.toLowerCase() === 'teacher') url = 'dashboard-teacher.html';
                else if (role.toLowerCase() === 'student') url = 'dashboard-student.html';
                else if (role.toLowerCase() === 'parent') url = 'dashboard-parents.html';
                window.location.href = url;
            });
        };
        
        const generateDetailedResultsPDF = () => {
            try {
                // Check if jsPDF is available (using CDN version)
                if (typeof jspdf === 'undefined' || typeof window.jspdf.jsPDF === 'undefined') {
                    throw new Error('jsPDF library not loaded. Please check your internet connection.');
                }
                
                const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf;
                const doc = new jsPDF();
                
                // Add school header
                doc.setFontSize(18);
                doc.setTextColor(40, 40, 40);
                doc.text("ST. CHRISTOPHER SCHOOL", 105, 20, { align: 'center' });
                doc.setFontSize(14);
                doc.text("OFFICIAL EXAMINATION RESULT", 105, 30, { align: 'center' });
                
                // Add student information
                doc.setFontSize(12);
                doc.text(`Candidate: ${state.studentData.name || state.studentData.username || 'N/A'}`, 20, 50);
                doc.text(`Class: ${state.studentData.class || state.studentData.grade || 'N/A'}`, 20, 60);
                doc.text(`Subject: ${state.testData.testName || 'N/A'}`, 20, 70);
                doc.text(`Date: ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}`, 20, 80);
                
                // Create results table
                const tableColumns = ["Q#", "Your Answer", "Correct Answer", "Status"];
                const tableRows = state.questions.map((q, i) => {
                    const selected = state.userAnswers[i] || "Not answered";
                    const correct = q.correctAnswer;
                    const isCorrect = selected === correct;
                    
                    return [
                        i + 1,
                        selected,
                        correct,
                        isCorrect ? "✓ Correct" : "✗ Incorrect"
                    ];
                });
                
                // Add table to PDF - using the autoTable plugin
                doc.autoTable({ 
                    head: [tableColumns], 
                    body: tableRows, 
                    startY: 90,
                    theme: 'grid',
                    headStyles: {
                        fillColor: [2, 1, 105],
                        textColor: 255,
                        fontStyle: 'bold'
                    },
                    alternateRowStyles: {
                        fillColor: [240, 240, 240]
                    },
                    columnStyles: {
                        3: { fontStyle: 'bold' } // Status column
                    },
                    didDrawCell: (data) => {
                        // Color code correct/incorrect answers
                        if (data.section === 'body' && data.column.index === 3) {
                            const isCorrect = data.cell.raw === "✓ Correct";
                            doc.setTextColor(isCorrect ? 0 : 255, isCorrect ? 128 : 0, 0);
                        }
                    }
                });
                
                // Add summary
                const finalY = doc.lastAutoTable.finalY + 10;
                const aggregate = Math.round((state.score / state.questions.length) * 100);
                
                doc.setFontSize(14);
                doc.setTextColor(0, 0, 0);
                doc.text("PERFORMANCE SUMMARY", 20, finalY);
                
                doc.setFontSize(12);
                doc.text(`Total Questions: ${state.questions.length}`, 20, finalY + 10);
                doc.text(`Correct Answers: ${state.score}`, 20, finalY + 20);
                doc.text(`Percentage Score: ${aggregate}%`, 20, finalY + 30);
                
                // Add grade interpretation
                let interpretation = "";
                if (aggregate >= 80) interpretation = "EXCELLENT";
                else if (aggregate >= 70) interpretation = "VERY GOOD";
                else if (aggregate >= 60) interpretation = "GOOD";
                else if (aggregate >= 50) interpretation = "PASS";
                else interpretation = "FAIL";
                
                doc.setFontSize(12);
                doc.setTextColor(0, 0, 0);
                doc.text(`Remarks: ${interpretation}`, 20, finalY + 45);
                
                // Add footer
                doc.setFontSize(10);
                doc.setTextColor(100, 100, 100);
                doc.text("Generated by TestHall Examination System", 105, doc.internal.pageSize.height - 10, { align: 'center' });

                // Save the PDF
                const fileName = `Exam_Result_${state.testData.testName || 'Exam'}_${state.studentData.name || 'Student'}.pdf`
                    .replace(/\s+/g, '_') // Replace spaces with underscores
                    .replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/g, ''); // Remove special characters
                
                doc.save(fileName);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("PDF Generation Failed:", error);
                alert("Could not generate PDF. " + error.message + "\n\nPlease check your internet connection and try again.");
            }
        };

        // --- START THE APPLICATION ---
        initializeExam();
    });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <meta name="theme-color" content="#020169">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">

            ];

            /**
             * Displays games based on current search and filter criteria
             */
            function displayGames() {
                const searchTerm = searchInput.value.toLowerCase();
                const category = categoryFilter.value;
                
                // Filter games based on search and category
                const filteredGames = games.filter(game => {
                    const matchesSearch = game.title.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm) || 
                                        game.description.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm);
                    const matchesCategory = category === 'all' || game.category === category;
                    return matchesSearch && matchesCategory;
                });
                
                // Clear the game grid
                gameGrid.innerHTML = '';
                
                // Display filtered games or no results message
                if (filteredGames.length === 0) {
                    gameGrid.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="no-results">
                            <i class="fas fa-search"></i>
                            <h3>No games found</h3>
                            <p>Try adjusting your search or filter criteria</p>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    return;
                }
                
                // Create and append game cards
                filteredGames.forEach(game => {
                    const highScore = localStorage.getItem(game.scoreKey) || '0';
                    const lastScore = localStorage.getItem(game.lastScoreKey) || 'N/A';
                    
                    const card = document.createElement('div');
                    card.className = 'game-card';
                    card.dataset.category = game.category;
                    
                    card.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="game-header">
                            <div class="game-icon">
                                <i class="fas ${game.icon}"></i>
                            </div>
                            <h3 class="game-title">${game.title}</h3>
                            <span class="category-badge">${game.category}</span>
                        </div>
                        
                        <div class="game-body">
                            <p class="game-description">${game.description}</p>
                            
                            <div class="game-scores">
                                <div class="score-item">
                                    <div class="score-label">High Score</div>
                                    <div class="score-value">${highScore}</div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="score-item">
                                    <div class="score-label">Last Score</div>
                                    <div class="score-value">${lastScore}</div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            
                            <div class="game-actions">
                                <a href="${game.url}" class="play-btn">
                                    <i class="fas fa-play"></i> Play Now
                                </a>
                                <button class="reset-btn" onclick="resetScores('${game.scoreKey}', '${game.lastScoreKey}')">
                                    <i class="fas fa-redo"></i>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    
                    gameGrid.appendChild(card);
                });
            }
            
            /**
             * Resets scores for a specific game
             * @param {string} scoreKey - The localStorage key for high score
             * @param {string} lastScoreKey - The localStorage key for last score
             */
            window.resetScores = function(scoreKey, lastScoreKey) {
                if (confirm('Are you sure you want to reset your scores for this game?')) {
                    localStorage.removeItem(scoreKey);
                    localStorage.removeItem(lastScoreKey);
                    displayGames(); // Refresh the display
                    
                    // Show confirmation message
                    const confirmation = document.createElement('div');
                    confirmation.style.position = 'fixed';
                    confirmation.style.bottom = '20px';
                    confirmation.style.right = '20px';
                    confirmation.style.padding = '10px 20px';
                    confirmation.style.background = 'var(--success)';
                    confirmation.style.color = 'white';
                    confirmation.style.borderRadius = 'var(--border-radius)';
                    confirmation.style.zIndex = '1000';
                    confirmation.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-check"></i> Scores reset successfully!';
                    document.body.appendChild(confirmation);
                    
                    // Remove confirmation after 3 seconds
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        document.body.removeChild(confirmation);
                    }, 3000);
                }
            };
            
            /**
             * Initializes the game hall
             */
            function initGameHall() {
                // Display games initially
                displayGames();
                
                // Set up event listeners
                searchInput.addEventListener('input', displayGames);
                categoryFilter.addEventListener('change', displayGames);
                
                // Hide loading overlay
                setTimeout(() => {
                    loadingOverlay.style.opacity = '0';
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        loadingOverlay.style.display = 'none';
                    }, 500);
                }, 800);
            }
            
            // Initialize the game hall
            initGameHall();
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

            <h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2>
            <div class="faq-accordion">
                <details>
                    <summary>How do I take a Test or Exam?</summary>
                    <div class="faq-answer">
                        <p>To begin an assessment, navigate to either the "Test Library" or "Exam Library" from the side menu. Browse the list of available subjects and click on the one you wish to take. The test will start immediately.</p>
                    </div>
                </details>
                <details>
                    <summary>Where can I see my results?</summary>
                    <div class="faq-answer">
                        <p>After you submit a test or exam, you will be shown a results page. You can download a PDF of this result. Past results may be available on your Dashboard, depending on your user role.</p>
                    </div>
                </details>
                <details>
                    <summary>Can I change my answer after selecting it?</summary>
                    <div class="faq-answer">
                        <p>Yes. As long as you have not submitted the exam, you can navigate back to any question using the number panel at the bottom of the screen and change your selected option.</p>
                    </div>
                </details>
                 <details>
                    <summary>What is the difference between a Test and an Exam?</summary>
                    <div class="faq-answer">
                        <p>In our system, "Tests" are typically shorter assessments or practice quizzes, while "Exams" are formal, term-end assessments that contribute to your final grade.</p>
                    </div>
                </details>
            </div>

            <h2 style="margin-top: 40px;">Getting Started Guide</h2>
            <ol class="guide-steps">
                <li>
                    <strong>Log In or Register:</strong> Access the platform using your official school credentials. If you are a new user, you can register for temporary 60-minute access.
                </li>
                <li>
                    <strong>Navigate the Dashboard:</strong> Your dashboard is your central hub. From here, you can access all major sections of the platform.
                </li>

                    <strong>Select an Assessment:</strong> Go to the "Test Library" or "Exam Library" to find your desired subject. You can use the search bar to filter the list quickly.
                </li>

                    <strong>Take the Test:</strong> Answer each question carefully. Use the navigation panel to jump between questions if needed. Your progress is saved as you move to the next question.
                </li>

                    <strong>Submit and View Results:</strong> Once you are finished, click the "Submit Exam" button. Your results will be calculated and displayed instantly. Don't forget to download your PDF result slip!
                </li>
            </ol>
        </div>

    <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        // --- UI & NAVIGATION (Consistent with other pages) ---
        const hamburgerIcon = document.getElementById('hamburger-icon');
        const offCanvasMenu = document.getElementById('off-canvas-menu');
        hamburgerIcon.addEventListener('click', () => offCanvasMenu.classList.toggle('open'));

        const redirectToRoleDashboard = () => {
            const userRole = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserRole');
            let url = 'dashboard.html';
            if (userRole) {
                const r = userRole.toLowerCase();
                if (r === 'admin') url = 'dashboard-admin.html';
                else if (r === 'teacher') url = 'dashboard-teacher.html';
                else if (r === 'student') url = 'dashboard-student.html';
                else if (r === 'parent') url = 'dashboard-parents.html';
            }
            window.location.href = url;
        };
        
        // Add event listeners for all menu links
        document.getElementById('dashboard-link').addEventListener('click', redirectToRoleDashboard);
        document.getElementById('test-library-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'test-library.html');
        document.getElementById('exam-library-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'exam-library.html');
        document.getElementById('profile-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'profile.html');
        document.getElementById('classes-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'classes.html');
        document.getElementById('guide-faq-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'guide-FAQ.html');
        document.getElementById('logout-button').addEventListener('click', () => {
            localStorage.clear();
            window.location.href = 'login.html';
        });

        // --- PROFILE ICON ---
        const profileDisplayIcon = document.getElementById('user-image-display');
        const loggedInUserId = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserId');
        if (loggedInUserId) {
            fetch('../data/users.json')
        .then(response => {
            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
            }
            return response.json();
        })
        .then(users => {
            const tempUsers = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tempUsers')) || [];
            const allUsers = [...users, ...tempUsers];
            const currentUser = allUsers.find(user => user.id == loggedInUserId);
            
            if (currentUser) {
                const profilePicPath = currentUser.profilePicture ? currentUser.profilePicture : '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
                profileDisplayIcon.src = profilePicPath;
            } else {
                console.log('User not found');
                profileDisplayIcon.src = '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error loading profile data:', error);
            profileDisplayIcon.src = '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
        });
        
        }
        profileDisplayIcon.addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'profile.html');
    });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            // Array of possible redirect URLs
            const redirectUrls = [
                '../pages/profile.html',
                '../pages/dashboard.html',
                '../pages/about.html',
                '../pages/contact.html',
                '../pages/admission.html'
            ];

            // Randomly select one URL
            const randomIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * redirectUrls.length);
            const redirectUrl = redirectUrls[randomIndex];

            // Redirect after 3 seconds (matches CSS animation)
            setTimeout(() => {
                window.location.href = redirectUrl;
            }, 3000);
        });
    </script>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
const container = document.getElementById('container');
const toggleRegisterBtn = document.getElementById('toggle-register');
const toggleLoginBtn = document.getElementById('toggle-login');
const mobileRegisterLink = document.getElementById('mobile-register-link');
const mobileLoginLink = document.getElementById('mobile-login-link');
const loginForm = document.getElementById('login-form');
const signupForm = document.getElementById('signup-form');
const loginMessageBox = document.getElementById('login-message-box');
const showPasswordIcon = document.getElementById('show-password-icon');

const activateContainer = () => container.classList.add('active');
const deactivateContainer = () => container.classList.remove('active');
toggleRegisterBtn.addEventListener('click', activateContainer);
toggleLoginBtn.addEventListener('click', deactivateContainer);
mobileRegisterLink.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); activateContainer(); });
mobileLoginLink.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); deactivateContainer(); });

// ===================================================================
// *** THE CORRECTED REDIRECT LOGIC IS HERE ***
// ===================================================================
const redirectToDashboard = (role) => {
//only index.hrml is at the ROOT, Since login.html is not at the ROOT, all paths to pages must include '../pages/'
let url = '../pages/dashboard.html'; // Default fallback
const r = role ? role.toLowerCase() : '';
if (r === 'admin') url = '../pages/dashboard-admin.html';
else if (r === 'teacher') url = '../pages/dashboard-teacher.html';
else if (r === 'student') url = '../pages/dashboard-student.html';
else if (r === 'parent') url = '../pages/dashboard-parents.html';
window.location.href = url;
};

const showMessage = (element, text, type = 'error') => {
element.textContent = text;
element.className = `message-box ${type}`;
element.style.display = 'block';
};

const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
if (urlParams.get('reason') === 'unregistered') {
showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'CONTACT AN ADMINISTRATOR TO COMPLETE YOUR REGISTRATION.', 'info');
}

if (localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserId')) {
redirectToDashboard(localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserRole'));
return; 
}

loginForm.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
e.preventDefault();
const username = document.getElementById('login-username').value.trim();
const password = document.getElementById('login-password').value.trim();
if (!username || !password) return showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'Please enter username and password.');
try {
// Path is './' because login.html is at the root
const response = await fetch('../data/users.json');
if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Cannot fetch user data.');
const officialUsers = await response.json();
let user = officialUsers.find(u => u.username === username && u.password === password);
let isTemporaryUser = false;
if (!user) {
const tempUsers = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tempUsers')) || [];
user = tempUsers.find(u => u.username === username && u.password === password);
if (user) isTemporaryUser = true;
}
if (user) {
localStorage.setItem('loggedInUserId', user.id);
localStorage.setItem('loggedInUserRole', user.role);
localStorage.setItem('loggedInUsername', user.name || user.username);
if (isTemporaryUser) {
const sixtyMinutes = 60 * 60 * 1000;
localStorage.setItem('sessionExpiration', Date.now() + sixtyMinutes);
alert("DON'T FORGET TO FILL UP A PHYSICAL REGISTRATION SLIP AT THE E-LIBRARY. CONTACT AN ADMINISTRATOR TO COMPLETE YOUR REGISTRATION.");
} else {
alert(`Welcome, ${user.name || user.username}!`);
}
redirectToDashboard(user.role);
} else {
showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'Invalid username or password.');
}
} catch (error) {
console.error('Login failed:', error);
showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'An error occurred during login.');
}
});


signupForm.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const role = document.getElementById('signup-role').value;
    const gender = document.getElementById('signup-gender').value;
    const passcodeInput = document.getElementById('teacher-passcode');
    if (role === 'Teacher') {
        const today = new Date();
        const passcode = `${today.getDate().toString().padStart(2, '0')}${(today.getMonth() + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0')}${today.getFullYear()}`;
        if (passcodeInput.value !== passcode) {
            alert('Invalid teacher passcode.');
            return;
        }
    }
    const newUser = {
        id: `temp_${Date.now()}`,
        name: document.getElementById('signup-name').value.trim(),
        username: document.getElementById('signup-username').value.trim(),
        role: role,
        password: document.getElementById('signup-password').value.trim(),
        profilePicture: gender === 'Male' ? '../assets/profile picture/male.jpeg' : '../assets/profile picture/female.jpeg'
    };
    if (!newUser.name || !newUser.username || !newUser.role || !newUser.password || !gender) {
        return alert('Please fill all fields.');
    }
    let tempUsers = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tempUsers')) || [];
    tempUsers.push(newUser);
    localStorage.setItem('tempUsers', JSON.stringify(tempUsers));
    alert('Account created successfully! Please Sign In.');
    deactivateContainer();
});

document.getElementById('signup-role').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
    const role = e.target.value;
    const teacherPasscodeSection = document.getElementById('teacher-passcode-section');
    if (role === 'Teacher') {
        teacherPasscodeSection.style.display = 'block';
    } else {
        teacherPasscodeSection.style.display = 'none';
    }
});

showPasswordIcon.addEventListener('click', () => {
const loginPasswordInput = document.getElementById('login-password');
const isPassword = loginPasswordInput.type === 'password';
loginPasswordInput.type = isPassword ? 'text' : 'password';
showPasswordIcon.className = `fa-solid ${isPassword ? 'fa-eye-slash' : 'fa-eye'}`;
});


        // --- BACKGROUND IMAGE ROTATION WITH CORRECTED PATHS ---
        const backgroundImages = [
            '../assets/images/background/bg01.jpg', '../assets/images/background/bg02.jpg',
            '../assets/images/background/bg03.jpg', '../assets/images/background/bg04.jpg',
            '../assets/images/background/bg05.jpg', '../assets/images/background/bg06.jpg',
            '../assets/images/background/bg07.jpg', '../assets/images/background/bg08.jpg',
            '../assets/images/background/bg09.jpg', '../assets/images/background/bg10.jpg',
            '../assets/images/background/bg11.jpg', '../assets/images/background/bg12.jpg',
            '../assets/images/background/bg13.jpg', '../assets/images/background/bg14.jpg',
            '../assets/images/background/bg15.jpg', '../assets/images/background/bg16.jpg'
        ];



backgroundImages.forEach(src => { new Image().src = src; });
let currentIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * backgroundImages.length);
document.body.style.backgroundImage = `url('${backgroundImages[currentIndex]}')`;
setInterval(() => {
currentIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % backgroundImages.length;
document.body.style.backgroundImage = `url('${backgroundImages[currentIndex]}')`;
}, 5000);
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
const container = document.getElementById('container');
const toggleRegisterBtn = document.getElementById('toggle-register');
const toggleLoginBtn = document.getElementById('toggle-login');
const mobileRegisterLink = document.getElementById('mobile-register-link');
const mobileLoginLink = document.getElementById('mobile-login-link');
const loginForm = document.getElementById('login-form');
const signupForm = document.getElementById('signup-form');
const loginMessageBox = document.getElementById('login-message-box');
const showPasswordIcon = document.getElementById('show-password-icon');
const generateLinkBtn = document.getElementById('generate-link-btn');
const copyLinkBtn = document.getElementById('copy-link-btn');

const activateContainer = () => container.classList.add('active');
const deactivateContainer = () => container.classList.remove('active');
toggleRegisterBtn.addEventListener('click', activateContainer);
toggleLoginBtn.addEventListener('click', deactivateContainer);
mobileRegisterLink.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); activateContainer(); });
mobileLoginLink.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); deactivateContainer(); });

// ===================================================================
// *** THE CORRECTED REDIRECT LOGIC IS HERE ***
// ===================================================================
const redirectToDashboard = (role) => {
let url = '../pages/dashboard.html'; // Default fallback
const r = role ? role.toLowerCase() : '';
if (r === 'admin') url = '../pages/dashboard-admin.html';
else if (r === 'teacher') url = '../pages/dashboard-teacher.html';
else if (r === 'student') url = '../pages/dashboard-student.html';
else if (r === 'parent') url = '../pages/dashboard-parents.html';
window.location.href = url;
};

const showMessage = (element, text, type = 'error') => {
element.textContent = text;
element.className = `message-box ${type}`;
element.style.display = 'block';
};

// NEW: Check for URL credentials and auto-login
const checkUrlCredentials = async () => {
const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
const urlUsername = urlParams.get('user');
const urlPassword = urlParams.get('pass');
const urlRole = urlParams.get('role');
    
if (urlUsername && urlPassword) {
// Show loading message
showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'Logging in with provided credentials...', 'info');
    
try {
// Try to login with the URL credentials
const response = await fetch('../data/users.json');
if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Cannot fetch user data.');
const officialUsers = await response.json();
let user = officialUsers.find(u => u.username === urlUsername && u.password === urlPassword);
let isTemporaryUser = false;
        
if (!user) {
const tempUsers = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tempUsers')) || [];
user = tempUsers.find(u => u.username === urlUsername && u.password === urlPassword);
if (user) isTemporaryUser = true;
}
        
if (user) {
// Successfully logged in
localStorage.setItem('loggedInUserId', user.id);
localStorage.setItem('loggedInUserRole', user.role);
localStorage.setItem('loggedInUsername', user.name || user.username);
            
if (isTemporaryUser) {
const sixtyMinutes = 60 * 60 * 1000;
localStorage.setItem('sessionExpiration', Date.now() + sixtyMinutes);
}
            
// Remove credentials from URL for security
window.history.replaceState({}, document.title, window.location.pathname);
redirectToDashboard(user.role);
} else {
showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'Invalid credentials in URL.', 'error');
}
} catch (error) {
console.error('Auto-login failed:', error);
showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'Auto-login failed. Please try manual login.', 'error');
}
}
};

// NEW: Generate login link function
generateLinkBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
const username = document.getElementById('login-username').value.trim();
const password = document.getElementById('login-password').value.trim();
    
if (!username || !password) {
showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'Please enter username and password first.', 'error');
return;
}
    
// Create the login URL with credentials
const currentUrl = window.location.href.split('?')[0]; // Get base URL without existing params
const loginUrl = `${currentUrl}?user=${encodeURIComponent(username)}&pass=${encodeURIComponent(password)}`;
    
// Display and enable copying
generateLinkBtn.style.display = 'none';
copyLinkBtn.style.display = 'inline-block';
copyLinkBtn.setAttribute('data-url', loginUrl);
    
showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'Login link generated! Click "Copy Link" to share.', 'info');
});

// NEW: Copy link to clipboard
copyLinkBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
const loginUrl = copyLinkBtn.getAttribute('data-url');
    
navigator.clipboard.writeText(loginUrl).then(() => {
showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'Login link copied to clipboard!', 'info');
setTimeout(() => {
copyLinkBtn.style.display = 'none';
generateLinkBtn.style.display = 'inline-block';
loginMessageBox.style.display = 'none';
}, 2000);
}).catch(err => {
console.error('Failed to copy: ', err);
showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'Failed to copy link. Please try again.', 'error');
});
});

const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
if (urlParams.get('reason') === 'unregistered') {
showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'CONTACT AN ADMINISTRATOR TO COMPLETE YOUR REGISTRATION.', 'info');
}

if (localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserId')) {
redirectToDashboard(localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserRole'));
return; 
}

// Check for URL credentials on page load
checkUrlCredentials();

loginForm.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
e.preventDefault();
const username = document.getElementById('login-username').value.trim();
const password = document.getElementById('login-password').value.trim();
if (!username || !password) return showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'Please enter username and password.');
try {
// Path is './' because login.html is at the root
const response = await fetch('../data/users.json');
if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Cannot fetch user data.');
const officialUsers = await response.json();
let user = officialUsers.find(u => u.username === username && u.password === password);
let isTemporaryUser = false;
if (!user) {
const tempUsers = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tempUsers')) || [];
user = tempUsers.find(u => u.username === username && u.password === password);
if (user) isTemporaryUser = true;
}
if (user) {
localStorage.setItem('loggedInUserId', user.id);
localStorage.setItem('loggedInUserRole', user.role);
localStorage.setItem('loggedInUsername', user.name || user.username);
if (isTemporaryUser) {
const sixtyMinutes = 60 * 60 * 1000;
localStorage.setItem('sessionExpiration', Date.now() + sixtyMinutes);
alert("DON'T FORGET TO FILL UP A PHYSICAL REGISTRATION SLIP AT THE E-LIBRARY. CONTACT AN ADMINISTRATOR TO COMPLETE YOUR REGISTRATION.");
} else {
alert(`Welcome, ${user.name || user.username}!`);
}
redirectToDashboard(user.role);
} else {
showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'Invalid username or password.');
}
} catch (error) {
console.error('Login failed:', error);
showMessage(loginMessageBox, 'An error occurred during login.');
}
});

signupForm.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const role = document.getElementById('signup-role').value;
    const gender = document.getElementById('signup-gender').value;
    const passcodeInput = document.getElementById('teacher-passcode');
    if (role === 'Teacher') {
        const today = new Date();
        const passcode = `${today.getDate().toString().padStart(2, '0')}${(today.getMonth() + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0')}${today.getFullYear()}`;
        if (passcodeInput.value !== passcode) {
            alert('Invalid teacher passcode.');
            return;
        }
    }
    const newUser = {
        id: `temp_${Date.now()}`,
        name: document.getElementById('signup-name').value.trim(),
        username: document.getElementById('signup-username').value.trim(),
        role: role,
        password: document.getElementById('signup-password').value.trim(),
        profilePicture: gender === 'Male' ? '../assets/profile picture/male.jpeg' : '../assets/profile picture/female.jpeg'
    };
    if (!newUser.name || !newUser.username || !newUser.role || !newUser.password || !gender) {
        return alert('Please fill all fields.');
    }
    let tempUsers = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tempUsers')) || [];
    tempUsers.push(newUser);
    localStorage.setItem('tempUsers', JSON.stringify(tempUsers));
    alert('Account created successfully! Please Sign In.');
    deactivateContainer();
});

document.getElementById('signup-role').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
    const role = e.target.value;
    const teacherPasscodeSection = document.getElementById('teacher-passcode-section');
    if (role === 'Teacher') {
        teacherPasscodeSection.style.display = 'block';
    } else {
        teacherPasscodeSection.style.display = 'none';
    }
});

showPasswordIcon.addEventListener('click', () => {
const loginPasswordInput = document.getElementById('login-password');
const isPassword = loginPasswordInput.type === 'password';
loginPasswordInput.type = isPassword ? 'text' : 'password';
showPasswordIcon.className = `fa-solid ${isPassword ? 'fa-eye-slash' : 'fa-eye'}`;
});

// --- Background Image Rotation ---
const backgroundImages = ['../assets/images/background/bg1.jpg', '../assets/images/background/bg2.jpg', '../assets/images/background/bg3.jpg', '../assets/images/background/bg4.jpg'];
backgroundImages.forEach(src => { new Image().src = src; });
let currentIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * backgroundImages.length);
document.body.style.backgroundImage = `url('${backgroundImages[currentIndex]}')`;
setInterval(() => {
currentIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % backgroundImages.length;
document.body.style.backgroundImage = `url('${backgroundImages[currentIndex]}')`;
}, 5000);
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        // --- UI ELEMENTS ---
        const hamburgerIcon = document.getElementById('hamburger-icon');
        const offCanvasMenu = document.getElementById('off-canvas-menu');
        const profileDisplayIcon = document.getElementById('user-image-display');
        const logoutButton = document.getElementById('logout-button');

        // --- HAMBURGER MENU TOGGLE ---
        hamburgerIcon.addEventListener('click', () => offCanvasMenu.classList.toggle('open'));

        // --- SMART & REGULAR NAVIGATION LINKS ---
        // Get all the link elements by their new IDs
        const dashboardLink = document.getElementById('dashboard-link');
        const testLibraryLink = document.getElementById('test-library-link');
        const examLibraryLink = document.getElementById('exam-library-link');
        const profileLink = document.getElementById('profile-link');
        const classesLink = document.getElementById('classes-link');

        const redirectToRoleDashboard = () => {
            const userRole = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserRole');
            let url = 'dashboard.html'; // Fallback
            if (userRole) {
                const r = userRole.toLowerCase();
                if (r === 'admin') url = 'dashboard-admin.html';
                else if (r === 'teacher') url = 'dashboard-teacher.html';
                else if (r === 'student') url = 'dashboard-student.html';
                else if (r === 'parent') url = 'dashboard-parents.html';
            }
            window.location.href = url;
        };
        
        // Attach all click events
        dashboardLink.addEventListener('click', redirectToRoleDashboard);
        testLibraryLink.addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'test-library.html');
        examLibraryLink.addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'exam-library.html');
        profileLink.addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'profile.html');
        classesLink.addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'classes.html');

        // --- LOGOUT ---
        logoutButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
            localStorage.clear();
            // CORRECTED: all HTML file are in /pages/ to find login.html which is still in Pages folder
            window.location.href = 'login.html';
        });

        // --- UNIFIED FETCH & DISPLAY PROFILE DATA LOGIC ---
        // This is the one and only place we fetch user data
        const loggedInUserId = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserId');
        
        // The auth.js script already protects the page. This fetch is just for displaying data.
        if (loggedInUserId) {

fetch('../data/users.json')
    .then(response => {
        if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
        }
        return response.json();
    })
    .then(users => {
        let tempUsers = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tempUsers')) || [];
        const allUsers = [...users, ...tempUsers];
        const currentUser = allUsers.find(user => user.id == loggedInUserId);
        
        if (currentUser) {
            // Populate all profile fields
            document.getElementById('profile-name').textContent = currentUser.name || 'N/A';
            document.getElementById('profile-role').textContent = currentUser.role || 'N/A';
            document.getElementById('detail-name').textContent = currentUser.name || 'N/A';
            document.getElementById('detail-username').textContent = currentUser.username || 'N/A';
            document.getElementById('detail-id').textContent = currentUser.id || 'N/A';
            document.getElementById('detail-class').textContent = currentUser.class || 'N/A';

            // Set both profile pictures
            const profilePicPath = currentUser.profilePicture ? currentUser.profilePicture : '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
            document.getElementById('profile-pic-large').src = profilePicPath;
            profileDisplayIcon.src = profilePicPath;
        } else {
            console.log('User not found');
            document.getElementById('profile-name').textContent = 'User Not Found';
        }
    })
    .catch(error => {
        console.error('Error loading profile data:', error);
        document.getElementById('profile-name').textContent = 'Error Loading Data';
            // ... (rest of the code)
        });
    }
}); // Add this closing bracket
   </script>
</body>
</html>

  <select id="sort-select">
    <option value="name-asc">Name (A-Z)</option>
    <option value="name-desc">Name (Z-A)</option>
    <option value="role-asc">Role (A-Z)</option>
    <option value="role-desc">Role (Z-A)</option>

    <script>
        // Fetch users data from users.json file
const usersContainer = document.getElementById('users-tbody');
const searchInput = document.getElementById('search-input');
const sortSelect = document.getElementById('sort-select');

let users = [];

fetch('../data/users.json')
  .then(response => response.json())
  .then(data => {
    users = data;
    renderUsers(users);
  })
  .catch(error => console.error('Error fetching users:', error));

function renderUsers(users) {
  usersContainer.innerHTML = '';
  users.forEach(user => {
    const userCard = document.createElement('div');
    userCard.className = 'user-card';
    userCard.innerHTML = `
      <img src="${user.profilePicture}" alt="Profile Picture">
      <div class="user-details">
        <p>ID: ${user.id}</p>
        <p>Name: ${user.name}</p>
        <p>Email: ${user.email}</p>
        <p>Role: ${user.role}</p>
        <p>Class: ${user.class}</p>
      </div>
    `;
    usersContainer.appendChild(userCard);
  });
}

searchInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
  const searchValue = searchInput.value.toLowerCase();
  const filteredUsers = users.filter(user => {
    return user.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchValue) ||
           user.email.toLowerCase().includes(searchValue) ||
           user.role.toLowerCase().includes(searchValue) ||
           user.class.toLowerCase().includes(searchValue);
  });
  renderUsers(filteredUsers);
});

sortSelect.addEventListener('change', () => {
  const sortValue = sortSelect.value;
  const [sortField, sortOrder] = sortValue.split('-');
  const sortedUsers = users.slice().sort((a, b) => {
    if (sortField === 'name') {
      return sortOrder === 'asc' ? a.name.localeCompare(b.name) : b.name.localeCompare(a.name);
    } else if (sortField === 'role') {
      return sortOrder === 'asc' ? a.role.localeCompare(b.role) : b.role.localeCompare(a.role);
    } else if (sortField === 'class') {
      return sortOrder === 'asc' ? a.class.localeCompare(b.class) : b.class.localeCompare(a.class);
    }
  });
  renderUsers(sortedUsers);
});
    </script>
</body>
</html>

  <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      // --- Authentication Check ---
      const loggedInUserId = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserId');
      if (!loggedInUserId) {
        window.location.href = 'login.html';
        return;
      }

      // --- Logout Button Functionality ---
      const logoutButton = document.getElementById('logout-button');
      if (logoutButton) {
        logoutButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
          localStorage.removeItem('loggedInUserId');
          localStorage.removeItem('loggedInUserRole');
          localStorage.removeItem('loggedInUsername');
          alert('You have been logged out.');
          window.location.href = 'login.html';
        });
      }

      // --- Profile Picture Display ---
      const profileDisplayIcon = document.getElementById('user-image-display');
      let studentData = {}; // Initialize studentData

      fetch('../data/users.json')
          .then(response => {
              if (!response.ok) {
                  throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
              }
              return response.json();
          })
          .then(data => {
              if (Array.isArray(data)) {
                  studentData = data.find(user => user.id == loggedInUserId);
                  if (!studentData) {
                      console.warn(`User with ID ${loggedInUserId} not found in users.json. Using generic student data.`);
                      studentData = { id: loggedInUserId, name: 'Generic Student', username: 'generic.user', 'date of birth': 'Unknown', role: 'Student' };
                  }
              } else {
                  studentData = data;
              }

              if (profileDisplayIcon && studentData) {
                  const profilePicPath = studentData.profilePicture && studentData.profilePicture !== '' 
                                         ? studentData.profilePicture 
                                         : '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
                  profileDisplayIcon.src = profilePicPath;
                  profileDisplayIcon.alt = `${studentData.name}'s Profile Picture`;
                  profileDisplayIcon.title = `Go to ${studentData.name}'s Profile`;
                  profileDisplayIcon.style.cursor = 'pointer';
                  profileDisplayIcon.onclick = () => {
                      window.location.href = 'profile.html';
                  };
              }
          })
          .catch(error => {
              console.error('Error loading student data:', error);
              if (profileDisplayIcon) {
                  profileDisplayIcon.src = '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
                  profileDisplayIcon.alt = 'Default Profile Picture';
                  profileDisplayIcon.title = 'Default Profile';
                  profileDisplayIcon.onclick = null;
              }
          });

      // --- Dynamic Test List Generation ---
      const testListElement = document.getElementById('test-list');

      // This array should match the base names of your JSON test files in the '../data/' folder.
      // You could potentially fetch a manifest JSON file here if you had many tests.
      const availableTests = [
        { fileName: 'test_phe_tulip', displayName: 'B⁸ 2nd TERM PHE' },
        { fileName: 'test_phe_sunflower', displayName: 'B⁹ 2nd TERM PHE' },
        { fileName: 'test_ict_lavender', displayName: 'ICT Lavender Test' },
        { fileName: 'test_ict_tulip', displayName: 'ICT Tulip Test' },
        { fileName: 'test_phe_118', displayName: 'First Aid Knowledge Test' },
        { fileName: 'test_phe_lavender', displayName: 'B⁷ 2nd TERM PHE' },
        { fileName: 'tests', displayName: 'Mock Examination' },
        { fileName: ' agriculture-test-system', displayName: 'SS2 1st test Agric Sci' },
        { fileName: 'B7L-3rd', displayName: 'B⁷ 3rd TERM PHE' },
        { fileName: 'third_term_basic_eight_phe', displayName: 'B⁸ 3rd TERM PHE' }
        // Add more tests here as you create their JSON files
      ];

      if (testListElement) {
        testListElement.innerHTML = ''; // Clear any static content
        availableTests.forEach(test => {
          const listItem = document.createElement('li');
          const anchor = document.createElement('a');
          anchor.href = `test-taking.html?test=${test.fileName}`; // Correct link with query parameter
          anchor.textContent = test.displayName;
          listItem.appendChild(anchor);
          testListElement.appendChild(listItem);
        });
      }
    });
  </script>
