            <div id="results-wrapper" style="display: none;"></div>
        </div>
    </main>
  
    <img id="user-image-display" src="../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg" alt="User Profile" class="profile-display-icon">
    <footer>&copy; 2024 TestHall</footer>


<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    // --- SECTION 1: AUTHENTICATION GUARD ---
    const loggedInUserId = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserId');
    if (!loggedInUserId) {
        window.location.href = 'login.html';
        return;
    }
    
    const expirationTime = localStorage.getItem('sessionExpiration');
    if (expirationTime && Date.now() > parseInt(expirationTime, 10)) {
        localStorage.clear();
        alert('Your temporary session has expired.');
        window.location.href = 'login.html?reason=unregistered';
        return;
    }
    
    // Get profile icon element
    const profileDisplayIcon = document.getElementById('user-image-display');
    
    // Try to get user data from localStorage first
    let studentData = {};
    const loggedInUserString = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUser');
    
    if (loggedInUserString) {
        try {
            studentData = JSON.parse(loggedInUserString);
            
            // Set profile picture if available
            if (profileDisplayIcon && studentData.profilePicture) {
                const profilePicPath = studentData.profilePicture !== '' 
                    ? `../${studentData.profilePicture}` 
                    : '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
                profileDisplayIcon.src = profilePicPath;
                profileDisplayIcon.alt = `${studentData.name}'s Profile Picture`;
                profileDisplayIcon.title = `Go to ${studentData.name}'s Profile`;
                profileDisplayIcon.style.cursor = 'pointer';
                profileDisplayIcon.onclick = () => {
                    window.location.href = 'profile.html';
                };
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('Error parsing user data from localStorage:', e);
        }
    }
    
    // If we don't have complete user data, try to fetch from users.json
    if (!studentData.name) {
        fetch('../data/users.json')
            .then(response => {
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
                return response.json();
            })
            .then(data => {
                if (Array.isArray(data)) {
                    const foundUser = data.find(user => user.id == loggedInUserId);
                    if (foundUser) {
                        studentData = foundUser;
                        
                        // Update profile picture
                        if (profileDisplayIcon) {
                            const profilePicPath = studentData.profilePicture && studentData.profilePicture !== '' 
                                ? `../${studentData.profilePicture}` 
                                : '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg';
                            profileDisplayIcon.src = profilePicPath;
                            profileDisplayIcon.alt = `${studentData.name}'s Profile Picture`;
                            profileDisplayIcon.title = `Go to ${studentData.name}'s Profile`;
                            profileDisplayIcon.style.cursor = 'pointer';
                            profileDisplayIcon.onclick = () => {
                                window.location.href = 'profile.html';
                            };
                        }
                    } else {
                        console.warn(`User with ID ${loggedInUserId} not found in users.json. Using minimal student data.`);
                        studentData = { 
                            id: loggedInUserId, 
                            name: localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserName') || 'Student', 
                            username: 'student.user', 
                            role: 'Student' 
                        };
                    }
                }
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error loading student data:', error);
                // Fallback to minimal data
                studentData = { 
                    id: loggedInUserId, 
                    name: localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserName') || 'Student', 
                    username: 'student.user', 
                    role: 'Student' 
                };
            });
    }

    // --- SECTION 2: ELEMENT REFERENCES & STATE ---
    const ui = {
        title: document.getElementById('exam-title'),
        examWrapper: document.getElementById('exam-questions-wrapper'),
        summaryWrapper: document.getElementById('submission-summary-wrapper'),
        resultsWrapper: document.getElementById('results-wrapper'),
        form: document.getElementById('test-form'),
        navPanel: document.getElementById('question-navigation-panel'),
        prevBtn: document.getElementById('prev-btn'),
        nextBtn: document.getElementById('next-btn'),
        finishBtn: document.getElementById('finish-exam-btn'),
        goBackBtn: document.getElementById('go-back-to-exam-btn'),
        submitBtn: document.getElementById('final-submit-btn'),
        hamburger: document.getElementById('hamburger-icon'),
        menu: document.getElementById('off-canvas-menu'),
        profileIcon: document.getElementById('user-image-display'),
        summaryTableContainer: document.getElementById('summary-table-container')
    };
    
    let state = { 
        questions: [], 
        userAnswers: [], 
        studentData: studentData, // Use the studentData we already loaded
        testData: {}, 
        score: 0, 
        currentIndex: 0 
    };

    // --- SECTION 3: INITIALIZATION ---
    const initializeExam = async () => {
        try {
            const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const testFileName = urlParams.get('test');
            if (!testFileName) throw new Error("No exam file specified.");
            
            // Use the studentData we already loaded instead of trying to get from localStorage again
            if (!state.studentData.name) {
                // If we still don't have user data, try to get from localStorage as fallback
                const loggedInUserString = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUser');
                if (loggedInUserString) {
                    state.studentData = JSON.parse(loggedInUserString);
                } else {
                    // Final fallback - create minimal user object
                    state.studentData = {
                        id: loggedInUserId,
                        name: localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserName') || 'Student',
                        class: 'Unknown'
                    };
                }
            }

            const examResponse = await fetch(`../data/${testFileName}.json`);
            if (!examResponse.ok) throw new Error(`Could not load exam file.`);
            state.testData = await examResponse.json();
            
            state.questions = state.testData.questions || [];
            state.userAnswers = new Array(state.questions.length).fill(null);
            ui.title.textContent = state.testData.testName || 'Exam';
            
            setupUI();
            generateQuestionNavPanel();
            displayQuestion(0);

        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Initialization Failed:", error);
            ui.title.textContent = "Error";
            ui.examWrapper.innerHTML = `<p style="color: red; text-align: center;">${error.message}</p>`;
        }
    };
    
    // --- SECTION 4: UI SETUP AND EVENT LISTENERS ---
    const setupUI = () => {
        ui.hamburger.addEventListener('click', () => ui.menu.classList.toggle('open'));
        
        if (state.studentData.profilePicture) {
            ui.profileIcon.src = `../${state.studentData.profilePicture}`;
        }
        ui.profileIcon.addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'profile.html');
        
        const redirectToRoleDashboard = () => {
            const role = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserRole') || state.studentData.role || 'student';
            let url = 'dashboard.html';
            if (role.toLowerCase() === 'admin') url = 'dashboard-admin.html';
            else if (role.toLowerCase() === 'teacher') url = 'dashboard-teacher.html';
            else if (role.toLowerCase() === 'student') url = 'dashboard-student.html';
            else if (role.toLowerCase() === 'parent') url = 'dashboard-parents.html';
            window.location.href = url;
        };
        
        document.getElementById('dashboard-link').addEventListener('click', redirectToRoleDashboard);
        document.getElementById('exam-library-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'exam-library.html');
        document.getElementById('profile-link').addEventListener('click', () => window.location.href = 'profile.html');
        document.getElementById('logout-button').addEventListener('click', () => { 
            localStorage.clear(); 
            window.location.href = 'login.html'; 
        });
        
        ui.prevBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { 
            if (state.currentIndex > 0) { 
                saveCurrentAnswer(); 
                displayQuestion(state.currentIndex - 1); 
            } 
        });
        
        ui.nextBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { 
            if (state.currentIndex < state.questions.length - 1) { 
                saveCurrentAnswer(); 
                displayQuestion(state.currentIndex + 1); 
            } 
        });
        
        ui.finishBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { 
            saveCurrentAnswer(); 
            showSubmissionSummary(); 
        });
        
        ui.goBackBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { 
            ui.summaryWrapper.style.display = 'none'; 
            ui.examWrapper.style.display = 'block'; 
            ui.title.textContent = state.testData.testName; 
        });
        
        ui.submitBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            ui.summaryWrapper.style.display = 'none';
            ui.resultsWrapper.style.display = 'block';
            ui.title.textContent = "Final Result";
            ui.hamburger.style.display = 'block';
            calculateAndShowResults();
            setTimeout(() => alert("Your results have been successfully submitted."), 1000);
        });
    };
    
    // --- SECTION 5: DYNAMIC UI FUNCTIONS ---
    const displayQuestion = (index) => {
        state.currentIndex = index;
        const q = state.questions[index];
        const imageHTML = (q.imageURL && q.imageURL.trim() !== "") 
            ? `<img src="../${q.imageURL.replace('./', '')}" alt="Question Image" class="question-image">` 
            : '';
        
        const optionsHTML = q.options.map((opt, i) => 
            `<li>
                <label>
                    <input type="radio" name="q${index}" value="${opt}" ${state.userAnswers[index] === opt ? 'checked' : ''}> 
                    ${String.fromCharCode(65+i)}. ${opt}
                </label>
            </li>`
        ).join('');
        
        ui.form.innerHTML = `
            <div class="question-container">
                <p><strong>Question ${index + 1}:</strong> ${q.question}</p> 
                ${imageHTML} 
                <ul>${optionsHTML}</ul>
                <div class="question-actions">
                    <button type="button" class="clear-answer-btn">Clear My Answer</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
        
        updateNavigationButtons();
        updateQuestionNavPanel();
        
        ui.form.querySelector('.clear-answer-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            state.userAnswers[state.currentIndex] = null;
            displayQuestion(state.currentIndex);
        });
    };
    
    const updateNavigationButtons = () => {
        ui.prevBtn.disabled = state.currentIndex === 0;
        ui.nextBtn.disabled = state.currentIndex === state.questions.length - 1;
    };
    
    const generateQuestionNavPanel = () => {
        ui.navPanel.innerHTML = state.questions.map((_, i) => 
            `<button class="nav-question-btn">${i + 1}</button>`
        ).join('');
        
        ui.navPanel.querySelectorAll('.nav-question-btn').forEach((btn, i) => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => { 
                saveCurrentAnswer(); 
                displayQuestion(i); 
            });
        });
    };
    
    const updateQuestionNavPanel = () => {
        ui.navPanel.querySelectorAll('.nav-question-btn').forEach((btn, i) => {
            btn.classList.remove('current', 'answered');
            if (state.userAnswers[i] !== null) btn.classList.add('answered');
            if (i === state.currentIndex) btn.classList.add('current');
        });
    };
    
    const saveCurrentAnswer = () => {
        const selected = ui.form.querySelector(`input[name="q${state.currentIndex}"]:checked`);
        state.userAnswers[state.currentIndex] = selected ? selected.value : null;
    };

    // --- SECTION 6: WORKFLOW & RESULTS FUNCTIONS ---
    const showSubmissionSummary = () => {
        ui.examWrapper.style.display = 'none';
        ui.summaryWrapper.style.display = 'block';
        ui.title.textContent = 'Exam Submission Summary';
        
        const tableHTML = `
            <table class="summary-table">
                <thead>
                    <tr>
                        <th>Question</th>
                        <th>Status</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                    ${state.userAnswers.map((ans, i) => `
                        <tr>
                            <td>${i + 1}</td>
                            <td>
                                ${ans !== null 
                                    ? '<span class="status-answered">ANSWERED</span>' 
                                    : '<span class="status-unanswered">NOT ANSWERED</span>'}
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    `).join('')}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        `;
        
        ui.summaryTableContainer.innerHTML = tableHTML;
    };

    const calculateAndShowResults = () => {
        state.score = state.userAnswers.reduce((acc, ans, i) => 
            acc + (ans === state.questions[i].correctAnswer ? 1 : 0), 0);
        
        const percentage = state.questions.length > 0 
            ? Math.round((state.score / state.questions.length) * 100) 
            : 0;

        ui.resultsWrapper.innerHTML = `
            <div class="detailed-results-preview">
                <section class="header-section">
                    <div><img src="../assets/images/logo.png" alt="School Logo" style="max-width:80px;"></div>
                    <div><h2>St. Christopher School</h2><p style="font-weight: bold;">EXAMINATION RESULT</p></div>
                </section>
                <section>
                    <h2>Candidate & Exam Information</h2>
                    <div class="student-info">
                        <div>
                            <p><strong>Name:</strong> ${state.studentData.name || 'N/A'}</p>
                            <p><strong>Class:</strong> ${state.studentData.class || 'N/A'}</p>
                            <p><strong>Subject:</strong> ${state.testData.testName || 'N/A'}</p>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <img src="${state.studentData.profilePicture 
                                ? `../${state.studentData.profilePicture}` 
                                : '../assets/profile_pictures/default.jpeg'}" 
                                alt="Profile" class="profile-pic-preview">
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </section>
                <section class="summary-section">
                    <h2>Performance Summary</h2>
                    <p>Score: ${state.score} / ${state.questions.length}</p>
                    <p>Percentage: ${percentage}%</p>
                </section>
                <section class="action-buttons-container">
                    <button id="download-pdf-button" class="download-pdf-button">Download Detailed PDF</button>
                    <a id="dashboard-redirect-link" href="#" class="dashboard-link-button">Back to Dashboard</a>
                </section>
            </div>
        `;
        
        document.getElementById('download-pdf-button').addEventListener('click', generateDetailedResultsPDF);
        document.getElementById('dashboard-redirect-link').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const role = localStorage.getItem('loggedInUserRole') || state.studentData.role || 'student';
            let url = 'dashboard.html';
            if (role.toLowerCase() === 'admin') url = 'dashboard-admin.html';
            else if (role.toLowerCase() === 'teacher') url = 'dashboard-teacher.html';
            else if (role.toLowerCase() === 'student') url = 'dashboard-student.html';
            else if (role.toLowerCase() === 'parent') url = 'dashboard-parents.html';
            window.location.href = url;
        });
    };
    
    const generateDetailedResultsPDF = () => {
        try {
            if (!window.jspdf || !window.jspdf.jsPDF.autoTable) {
                throw new Error('jsPDF plugins not loaded.');
            }
            
            const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf;
            const doc = new jsPDF();
            
            doc.setFontSize(18).text("St. Christopher School - Official Exam Result", 105, 15, { align: 'center' });
            doc.setFontSize(11);
            doc.text(`Candidate: ${state.studentData.name || 'N/A'}`, 14, 25);
            doc.text(`Subject: ${state.testData.testName || 'N/A'}`, 14, 32);
            
            const tableColumns = ["#", "Selected Answer", "Correct Answer"];
            const tableRows = state.questions.map((q, i) => {
                const selected = state.userAnswers[i] ? state.userAnswers[i] : "NOT ANSWERED";
                return [i + 1, selected, q.correctAnswer];
            });
            
            doc.autoTable({ 
                head: [tableColumns], 
                body: tableRows, 
                startY: 45,
                theme: 'grid',
                headStyles: {
                    fillColor: [2, 1, 105], // Dark blue color similar to your primary color
                    textColor: 255
                }
            });
            
            const finalY = doc.lastAutoTable.finalY || 60;
            const aggregate = Math.round((state.score / state.questions.length) * 100);
            doc.setFontSize(12).text(`Aggregate Score: ${aggregate}%`, 14, finalY + 15);

            doc.save(`Detailed_Result_${state.studentData.name || 'Student'}.pdf`);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("PDF Generation Failed:", error);
            alert("Could not generate PDF. " + error.message);
        }
    };

    // --- START THE APPLICATION ---
    initializeExam();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

    <!-- 
      STEP 2: This second script uses the library we just pasted above.
      It finds the input field and the div and generates the QR code.
    -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        var qrcode = null; // Variable to hold the QR code object

        function generateQrCode() {
            var url = document.getElementById("urlInput").value.trim();
            var qrCodeContainer = document.getElementById("qrcode");

            // Don't generate if the input is empty
            if (!url) {
                alert("Please enter a URL");
                return;
            }

            // Clear the previous QR code if it exists
            qrCodeContainer.innerHTML = "";

            // Create a new QR code
            // The "QRCode" function is available because of the giant script block above
            qrcode = new QRCode(qrCodeContainer, {
                text: url,
                width: 256,
                height: 256,
                colorDark: "#000000",
                colorLight: "#ffffff",
                correctLevel: QRCode.CorrectLevel.H
            });
        }

        // Generate a QR code for the default value on page load
        window.onload = generateQrCode;
    </script>

</body>
</html>

    <!-- 
      SECTION 2: The Generator Script
      This second, smaller script USES the library from above to find your
      input field and generate the QR code in the 'qrcode' div.
    -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        var qrcode = null;

        function generateQrCode() {
            var url = document.getElementById("urlInput").value.trim();
            var qrCodeContainer = document.getElementById("qrcode");

            if (!url) {
                alert("Please enter a URL or text");
                qrCodeContainer.innerHTML = "";
                return;
            }

            qrCodeContainer.innerHTML = "";

            qrcode = new QRCode(qrCodeContainer, {
                text: url,
                width: 256,
                height: 256,
                colorDark: "#000000",
                colorLight: "#ffffff",
                correctLevel: QRCode.CorrectLevel.H
            });
        }

        // Generate a QR code for the default value when the page loads
        window.onload = generateQrCode;
    </script>

</body>

    <title>SCS User Manager (Admin Tool)</title>
    <style>
        :root { --primary-color: #020169; --accent-color: #FEC009; --error-color: #dc3545; --success-color: #28a745; }
        body { font-family: Arial, sans-serif; background-color: #f0f2f5; margin: 20px; }

<body>
    <div class="container">
        <h1>SCS User Manager (ADMIN TOOL)</h1>
        <p><strong>Important:</strong> This tool modifies your live <code>users.json</code> file. Use with care.</p>
        
        <h2>Current Registered Users</h2>
        <table id="users-table">
            <thead><tr><th>ID</th><th>Name</th><th>Username</th><th>Role</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody><!-- User data will be loaded here --></tbody>
        </table>

                    <div><label for="username">Username</label><input type="text" id="username" required></div>
                    <div><label for="password">Password</label><input type="text" id="password" required></div>
                    <div>

                        <select id="role" required>
                            <option value="Student">Student</option>
                            <option value="Teacher">Teacher</option>
                            <option value="Parent">Parent</option>
                            <option value="Admin">Admin</option>
                        </select>

                <button type="button" id="cancel-edit-btn" style="display: none;">Cancel Edit</button>

        <div id="output-container">
            <h2>Updated <code>users.json</code> Content</h2>
            <p>1. Copy the text below. <br> 2. Open <code>data/users.json</code> in your project. <br> 3. Paste this content, save the file, and upload it to GitHub.</p>

    <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        const tableBody = document.querySelector('#users-table tbody');
        const userForm = document.getElementById('user-form');
        const jsonOutput = document.getElementById('json-output');
        const formTitle = document.getElementById('form-title');
        const submitBtn = document.getElementById('submit-btn');
        const cancelEditBtn = document.getElementById('cancel-edit-btn');
        const userIdInput = document.getElementById('user-id');
        const nameInput = document.getElementById('name');
        const usernameInput = document.getElementById('username');
        const passwordInput = document.getElementById('password');
        const roleInput = document.getElementById('role');

        let users = [];

        // Load initial users from the local file
        const loadUsers = async () => {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('../data/users.json');
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error('File not found. Make sure users.json is in the /data folder.');
                users = await response.json();
                renderTable();
                updateJsonOutput();
            } catch (error) {
                alert(`Error loading users.json: ${error.message}`);
            }
        };

        // Render the user table
        const renderTable = () => {
            tableBody.innerHTML = '';
            users.forEach(user => {
                const row = tableBody.insertRow();
                row.innerHTML = `
                    <td>${user.id}</td>
                    <td>${user.name}</td>
                    <td>${user.username}</td>
                    <td>${user.role}</td>
                    <td>
                        <button class="edit-btn" data-id="${user.id}">Edit</button>
                        <button class="delete-btn btn-danger" data-id="${user.id}">Delete</button>
                    </td>
                `;
            });
        };

        // Update the output textarea
        const updateJsonOutput = () => {
            jsonOutput.value = JSON.stringify(users, null, 2);
        };

        // Handle form submission (Add or Update)
        userForm.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const id = userIdInput.value;
            const user = {
                name: nameInput.value,
                username: usernameInput.value,
                password: passwordInput.value,
                role: roleInput.value,
                // Add default properties for a new user
                class: "N/A",
                profilePicture: "assets/profile_picture/default.jpeg"
            };

            if (id) { // Updating an existing user
                const index = users.findIndex(u => u.id === id);
                users[index] = { ...users[index], ...user }; // Keep original ID and other props
            } else { // Adding a new user
                user.id = `user_${Date.now()}`; // Generate a unique ID
                users.push(user);
            }
            
            renderTable();
            updateJsonOutput();
            resetForm();
        });

        // Handle table clicks (Edit or Delete)
        tableBody.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const id = e.target.dataset.id;
            if (e.target.classList.contains('edit-btn')) {
                const user = users.find(u => u.id === id);
                formTitle.textContent = 'Edit User';
                submitBtn.textContent = 'Update User';
                cancelEditBtn.style.display = 'inline-block';
                userIdInput.value = user.id;
                nameInput.value = user.name;
                usernameInput.value = user.username;
                passwordInput.value = user.password;
                roleInput.value = user.role;
            } else if (e.target.classList.contains('delete-btn')) {
                if (confirm(`Are you sure you want to delete user: ${users.find(u=>u.id===id).name}?`)) {
                    users = users.filter(u => u.id !== id);
                    renderTable();
                    updateJsonOutput();
                }
            }
        });
        
        // Reset form to "Add User" mode
        const resetForm = () => {
            userForm.reset();
            userIdInput.value = '';
            formTitle.textContent = 'Add New User';
            submitBtn.textContent = 'Add User';
            cancelEditBtn.style.display = 'none';
        };

        cancelEditBtn.addEventListener('click', resetForm);
        
        // Initial load
        loadUsers();
    });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        // Fetch users data from users.json file
        fetch('../data/users.json')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(users => {
                const usersTbody = document.getElementById('users-tbody');
                users.forEach(user => {
                    const row = document.createElement('tr');
                    row.innerHTML = `
                        <td>${user.id}</td>
                        <td>${user.name}</td>
                        <td>${user.role}</td>
                    `;
                    row.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        document.getElementById('users-table').style.display = 'none';
                        document.getElementById('user-details').style.display = 'block';
                        showUserDetails(user);
                    });
                    usersTbody.appendChild(row);
                });
            })
            .catch(error => console.error('Error fetching users:', error));

        function showUserDetails(user) {
            document.getElementById('profile-picture').src = user.profilePicture;
            document.getElementById('name').textContent = `Name: ${user.name}`;
            document.getElementById('username').textContent = `Username: ${user.username}`;
            document.getElementById('date-of-birth').textContent = `Date of Birth: ${user['date of birth']}`;
            document.getElementById('email').textContent = `Email: ${user.email}`;
            document.getElementById('role').textContent = `Role: ${user.role}`;
            document.getElementById('class').textContent = `Class: ${user.class}`;

            document.getElementById('show-password-checkbox').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
                if (e.target.checked) {
                    const passcode = prompt('(Use Hint To Reset The passcode):');
                    if (passcode === getCurrentDate()) {
                        document.getElementById('password').textContent = `Password: ${user.password}`;
                    } else {
                        alert('Invalid passcode');
                        document.getElementById('show-password-checkbox').checked = false;
                    }
                } else {
                    document.getElementById('password').textContent = '*********';
                }
            });

            document.getElementById('hint-button').addEventListener('click', () => {
                const hints = [
                    'What has keys but can\'t open locks?',
                    'What starts with an E, ends with an E, but only contains one letter?',
                    'What is always coming but never arrives?'
                ];
                const randomHint = hints[Math.floor(Math.random() * hints.length)];
                const hintElement = document.getElementById('hint');
                if (hintElement.style.display === 'none') {
                    hintElement.style.display = 'block';
                    hintElement.textContent = randomHint;
                    document.getElementById('hint-button').textContent = 'Hide Hint';
                } else {
                    hintElement.style.display = 'none';
                    document.getElementById('hint-button').textContent = 'Show Hint';
                }
            });

            document.getElementById('back-button').addEventListener('click', () => {
                document.getElementById('users-table').style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('user-details').style.display = 'none';
            });
        }

        function getCurrentDate() {
            const date = new Date();
            const day = date.getDate().toString().padStart(2, '0');
            const month = (date.getMonth() + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0');
            const year = date.getFullYear();
            return `${day}${month}${year}`;
        }
    </script>
</body>

            width: 0%;
            transition: width 0.3s ease;
        }

        .stats-container {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-around;
            flex-wrap: wrap;
            margin-bottom: 30px;
            gap: 15px;
        }

        .stat-card {
            background-color: #f8f9fa;
            padding: 15px;
            border-radius: var(--border-radius);
            text-align: center;
            min-width: 150px;
            flex: 1;
        }

        .stat-number {
            font-size: 2rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            color: var(--primary);
            margin-bottom: 5px;
        }

        .stat-label {
            color: var(--text-light);
            font-size: 0.9rem;
        }

        .tree-container {
            background-color: #f8f9fa;
            padding: 20px;
            border-radius: var(--border-radius);
            max-height: 500px;
            overflow-y: auto;
            font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
            border: 1px solid #e9ecef;
        }

        .tree-item {
            padding: 5px 0;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            cursor: pointer;
            user-select: none;
        }

        .tree-item:hover {
            background-color: rgba(2, 1, 105, 0.05);
        }

        .folder {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: var(--primary);
        }

        .file {
            color: var(--text-dark);
        }

        .tree-icon {
            margin-right: 8px;
            width: 18px;
            text-align: center;
        }

        .tree-children {
            margin-left: 20px;
            display: block;
        }

        .tree-children.collapsed {
            display: none;
        }

        .empty-message {
            text-align: center;
            color: var(--text-light);
            padding: 30px;
            font-style: italic;
        }

        .action-buttons {
            display: flex;
            gap: 15px;
            justify-content: center;
            margin-top: 20px;
        }

        .btn {
            display: inline-flex;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
            padding: 12px 25px;
            border: none;
            border-radius: var(--border-radius);
            font-size: 1rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: var(--transition);
            gap: 8px;
        }

        .btn-primary {
            background-color: var(--primary);
            color: white;
        }

        .btn-primary:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-light);
        }

        .btn-accent {
            background-color: var(--accent);
            color: var(--primary);
        }

        .btn-accent:hover {
            background-color: var(--accent-dark);
        }

        .btn:disabled {
            background-color: #ccc;
            cursor: not-allowed;
        }

        .file-size {
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            color: var(--text-light);
            margin-left: 10px;
        }

        @media (max-width: 768px) {
            .container {
                padding: 20px;
            }
            
            h1 {
                font-size: 1.8rem;
            }
            
            .stats-container {
                flex-direction: column;
            }
            
            .action-buttons {
                flex-direction: column;
            }
            
            .btn {
                width: 100%;
            }
        }

        /* Animation for progress bar */
        @keyframes progressAnimation {
            0% { width: 0%; }
            100% { width: 100%; }
        }

        .progress-bar.animated {
            animation: progressAnimation 1.5s ease-in-out infinite;
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="container">
        <div class="header">
            <h1><i class="fas fa-file-archive"></i> ZIP File Structure Analyzer</h1>
            <p class="description">Upload a ZIP file to analyze its contents with a detailed tree view and comprehensive statistics</p>
        </div>

        <div class="upload-section">
            <div class="file-input-wrapper">
                <div class="file-input-button">
                    <i class="fas fa-cloud-upload-alt"></i> Choose ZIP File
                </div>
                <input type="file" id="fileInput" accept=".zip" />
            </div>
            <div class="file-name" id="fileName">No file selected</div>
            
            <div class="progress-container" id="progressContainer">
                <div class="progress-bar" id="progressBar"></div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="stats-container">
            <div class="stat-card">
                <div class="stat-number" id="fileCount">0</div>
                <div class="stat-label">Files</div>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-card">
                <div class="stat-number" id="folderCount">0</div>
                <div class="stat-label">Folders</div>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-card">
                <div class="stat-number" id="totalSize">0 B</div>
                <div class="stat-label">Total Size</div>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-card">
                <div class="stat-number" id="lastModified">-</div>
                <div class="stat-label">Last Modified</div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="tree-container" id="treeContainer">
            <div class="empty-message">
                <i class="fas fa-folder-open" style="font-size: 3rem; margin-bottom: 15px; display: block; opacity: 0.5;"></i>
                Upload a ZIP file to view its structure
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="action-buttons">
            <button class="btn btn-primary" id="expandAllBtn" disabled>
                <i class="fas fa-expand"></i> Expand All
            </button>
            <button class="btn btn-primary" id="collapseAllBtn" disabled>
                <i class="fas fa-compress"></i> Collapse All
            </button>
            <button class="btn btn-accent" id="exportBtn" disabled>
                <i class="fas fa-download"></i> Export File List
            </button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- JSZip from CDN -->
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/jszip@3.10.1/dist/jszip.min.js"></script>
    
    <script>
        /**
         * ZIP File Structure Analyzer
         * 
         * This application allows users to upload ZIP files and analyze their contents
         * with a detailed tree view, file statistics, and export capabilities.
         */

        // DOM Element References
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
        const fileName = document.getElementById('fileName');
        const progressContainer = document.getElementById('progressContainer');
        const progressBar = document.getElementById('progressBar');
        const treeContainer = document.getElementById('treeContainer');
        const fileCountElem = document.getElementById('fileCount');
        const folderCountElem = document.getElementById('folderCount');
        const totalSizeElem = document.getElementById('totalSize');
        const lastModifiedElem = document.getElementById('lastModified');
        const expandAllBtn = document.getElementById('expandAllBtn');
        const collapseAllBtn = document.getElementById('collapseAllBtn');
        const exportBtn = document.getElementById('exportBtn');

        // Application State
        let fileTree = null;
        let fileCount = 0;
        let folderCount = 0;
        let totalSize = 0;
        let lastModifiedDate = null;

        /**
         * Initialize the application
         */
        function init() {
            // Add event listeners
            fileInput.addEventListener('change', handleFileSelect);
            expandAllBtn.addEventListener('click', expandAllFolders);
            collapseAllBtn.addEventListener('click', collapseAllFolders);
            exportBtn.addEventListener('click', exportFileList);
        }

        /**
         * Handle file selection from input
         */
        function handleFileSelect(event) {
            const file = event.target.files[0];
            if (!file || !file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.zip')) {
                showError('Please select a valid ZIP file');
                return;
            }

            fileName.textContent = file.name;
            resetStats();
            showProgress();
            
            // Read and process the ZIP file
            readZipFile(file);
        }

        /**
         * Read and process the ZIP file
         * @param {File} file - The ZIP file to process
         */
        function readZipFile(file) {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            
            reader.onloadstart = function() {
                progressBar.classList.add('animated');
            };
            
            reader.onprogress = function(event) {
                if (event.lengthComputable) {
                    const percentLoaded = Math.round((event.loaded / event.total) * 100);
                    progressBar.style.width = percentLoaded + '%';
                }
            };
            
            reader.onload = function(event) {
                JSZip.loadAsync(event.target.result)
                    .then(zip => {
                        processZipContents(zip);
                        hideProgress();
                    })
                    .catch(error => {
                        hideProgress();
                        showError('Error reading ZIP file: ' + error.message);
                        console.error(error);
                    });
            };
            
            reader.onerror = function() {
                hideProgress();
                showError('Error reading file');
            };
            
            reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
        }

        /**
         * Process the contents of the ZIP file
         * @param {JSZip} zip - The JSZip instance
         */
        function processZipContents(zip) {
            // Reset counters
            fileCount = 0;
            folderCount = 0;
            totalSize = 0;
            fileTree = {};
            
            // Get the last modified date from the first file
            let firstFile = null;
            
            // Process each file in the ZIP
            zip.forEach((relativePath, zipEntry) => {
                if (!firstFile) firstFile = zipEntry;
                
                const parts = relativePath.split('/');
                let currentLevel = fileTree;
                
                // Build the tree structure
                parts.forEach((part, index) => {
                    if (!part) return; // Skip empty parts
                    
                    if (index === parts.length - 1 && !zipEntry.dir) {
                        // It's a file
                        currentLevel[part] = {
                            type: 'file',
                            size: zipEntry._data.uncompressedSize,
                            compressedSize: zipEntry._data.compressedSize,
                            date: zipEntry.date
                        };
                        fileCount++;
                        totalSize += zipEntry._data.uncompressedSize;
                    } else {
                        // It's a folder
                        if (!currentLevel[part]) {
                            currentLevel[part] = {
                                type: 'folder',
                                children: {},
                                expanded: true // Start with folders expanded
                            };
                            if (index < parts.length - 1 || zipEntry.dir) {
                                folderCount++;
                            }
                        }
                        currentLevel = currentLevel[part].children;
                    }
                });
            });
            
            // Update the last modified date
            if (firstFile) {
                lastModifiedDate = firstFile.date;
            }
            
            // Update the UI
            updateStatistics();
            renderTree();
            enableButtons();
        }

        /**
         * Render the file tree
         */
        function renderTree() {
            if (!fileTree || Object.keys(fileTree).length === 0) {
                treeContainer.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-message">No files found in the ZIP archive</div>';
                return;
            }
            
            treeContainer.innerHTML = '';
            treeContainer.appendChild(createTreeElement(fileTree, 0));
        }

        /**
         * Create a tree element from the file structure
         * @param {Object} node - The current node in the file tree
         * @param {number} depth - The current depth in the tree
         * @returns {HTMLElement} The tree element
         */
        function createTreeElement(node, depth) {
            const container = document.createElement('div');
            
            for (const [name, item] of Object.entries(node)) {
                const isFolder = item.type === 'folder';
                const element = document.createElement('div');
                element.className = `tree-item ${isFolder ? 'folder' : 'file'}`;
                
                // Create icon and name
                const icon = document.createElement('span');
                icon.className = 'tree-icon';
                icon.innerHTML = isFolder ? 
                    (item.expanded ? '<i class="fas fa-folder-open"></i>' : '<i class="fas fa-folder"></i>') : 
                    '<i class="fas fa-file"></i>';
                
                const nameSpan = document.createElement('span');
                nameSpan.textContent = name;
                
                element.appendChild(icon);
                element.appendChild(nameSpan);
                
                // Add file size for files
                if (!isFolder) {
                    const sizeSpan = document.createElement('span');
                    sizeSpan.className = 'file-size';
                    sizeSpan.textContent = formatFileSize(item.size);
                    element.appendChild(sizeSpan);
                }
                
                // Add click handler for folders
                if (isFolder) {
                    element.addEventListener('click', () => toggleFolder(item, element));
                }
                
                container.appendChild(element);
                
                // Add children for expanded folders
                if (isFolder && item.expanded && item.children) {
                    const childrenContainer = document.createElement('div');
                    childrenContainer.className = 'tree-children';
                    childrenContainer.appendChild(createTreeElement(item.children, depth + 1));
                    container.appendChild(childrenContainer);
                }
            }
            
            return container;
        }

        /**
         * Toggle folder expansion
         * @param {Object} folder - The folder object
         * @param {HTMLElement} element - The folder element
         */
        function toggleFolder(folder, element) {
            folder.expanded = !folder.expanded;
            
            // Find the children container
            const childrenContainer = element.nextElementSibling;
            if (childrenContainer && childrenContainer.classList.contains('tree-children')) {
                childrenContainer.classList.toggle('collapsed', !folder.expanded);
                
                // Update folder icon
                const icon = element.querySelector('.tree-icon');
                icon.innerHTML = folder.expanded ? 
                    '<i class="fas fa-folder-open"></i>' : 
                    '<i class="fas fa-folder"></i>';
            }
        }

        /**
         * Expand all folders
         */
        function expandAllFolders() {
            setAllFoldersExpanded(fileTree, true);
            renderTree();
        }

        /**
         * Collapse all folders
         */
        function collapseAllFolders() {
            setAllFoldersExpanded(fileTree, false);
            renderTree();
        }

        /**
         * Recursively set the expanded state for all folders
         * @param {Object} node - The current node
         * @param {boolean} expanded - Whether to expand or collapse
         */
        function setAllFoldersExpanded(node, expanded) {
            for (const item of Object.values(node)) {
                if (item.type === 'folder') {
                    item.expanded = expanded;
                    setAllFoldersExpanded(item.children, expanded);
                }
            }
        }

        /**
         * Export the file list to a text file
         */
        function exportFileList() {
            if (!fileTree) return;
            
            let output = `ZIP File Structure Analysis Report\n`;
            output += `Generated on: ${new Date().toLocaleString()}\n`;
            output += `Total Files: ${fileCount}\n`;
            output += `Total Folders: ${folderCount}\n`;
            output += `Total Size: ${formatFileSize(totalSize)}\n\n`;
            output += `FILE LIST:\n`;
            output += generateFileListText(fileTree, '');
            
            // Create download link
            const blob = new Blob([output], { type: 'text/plain' });
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a = document.createElement('a');
            a.href = url;
            a.download = 'zip_contents_report.txt';
            document.body.appendChild(a);
            a.click();
            document.body.removeChild(a);
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        }

        /**
         * Generate text representation of the file list
         * @param {Object} node - The current node
         * @param {string} prefix - The prefix for indentation
         * @returns {string} The text representation
         */
        function generateFileListText(node, prefix) {
            let output = '';
            
            for (const [name, item] of Object.entries(node)) {
                if (item.type === 'folder') {
                    output += `${prefix}📁 ${name}/\n`;
                    output += generateFileListText(item.children, prefix + '  ');
                } else {
                    output += `${prefix}📄 ${name} (${formatFileSize(item.size)})\n`;
                }
            }
            
            return output;
        }

        /**
         * Format file size in human-readable format
         * @param {number} bytes - The size in bytes
         * @returns {string} Formatted size string
         */
        function formatFileSize(bytes) {
            if (bytes === 0) return '0 B';
            
            const k = 1024;
            const sizes = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
            const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
            
            return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + ' ' + sizes[i];
        }

        /**
         * Update statistics in the UI
         */
        function updateStatistics() {
            fileCountElem.textContent = fileCount.toLocaleString();
            folderCountElem.textContent = folderCount.toLocaleString();
            totalSizeElem.textContent = formatFileSize(totalSize);
            
            if (lastModifiedDate) {
                lastModifiedElem.textContent = lastModifiedDate.toLocaleDateString();
            } else {
                lastModifiedElem.textContent = 'Unknown';
            }
        }

        /**
         * Show progress indicator
         */
        function showProgress() {
            progressContainer.style.display = 'block';
            progressBar.style.width = '0%';
            progressBar.classList.add('animated');
            treeContainer.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-message"><i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Processing ZIP file...</div>';
        }

        /**
         * Hide progress indicator
         */
        function hideProgress() {
            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
            progressBar.classList.remove('animated');
        }

        /**
         * Enable action buttons
         */
        function enableButtons() {
            expandAllBtn.disabled = false;
            collapseAllBtn.disabled = false;
            exportBtn.disabled = false;
        }

        /**
         * Reset statistics
         */
        function resetStats() {
            fileCount = 0;
            folderCount = 0;
            totalSize = 0;
            lastModifiedDate = null;
            
            fileCountElem.textContent = '0';
            folderCountElem.textContent = '0';
            totalSizeElem.textContent = '0 B';
            lastModifiedElem.textContent = '-';
            
            expandAllBtn.disabled = true;
            collapseAllBtn.disabled = true;
            exportBtn.disabled = true;
        }

        /**
         * Show error message
         * @param {string} message - The error message
         */
        function showError(message) {
            treeContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-message" style="color: var(--danger);">
                <i class="fas fa-exclamation-circle"></i> ${message}
            </div>`;
        }

        // Initialize the application when the DOM is loaded
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
    </script>
</body>
</html>
